   16: [Fact] public void RecedingTrajectoryDoesNotInventFutureIntersection()

   18: [Fact] public void ProjectionExplicitlyDeclaresConstantVelocityBasis()

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\Entities\Certifications\E7H1A\Cert Tests\WildsServiceContextCertificationTests.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Services;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services;
using Xunit;

namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Certifications
{
    public sealed class WildsServiceContextCertificationTests
    {
        [Fact]
        public async Task SuccessProducesQueryableExecutionProvenance()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture();
            var provenance = new object();
            var pulseId = Guid.NewGuid();
            var result = await fixture.Service.ExecuteAsync("request", provenance, null, pulseId);
            Assert.Equal("request-result", result.Value);
            Assert.True(fixture.Journal.TryGet(result.ExecutionId, out var record));
            Assert.NotNull(record);
            Assert.Equal(WildsServiceExecutionOutcome.Succeeded, record!.Outcome);
            Assert.Equal(fixture.Service.Id, record.ServiceId);
            Assert.Equal(fixture.Service.Slug, record.ServiceSlug);
            Assert.Equal(pulseId, record.PulseId);
            Assert.Same(provenance, record.Provenance);
            Assert.NotNull(record.StartedAt);
            Assert.NotNull(record.CompletedAt);
            Assert.NotNull(record.Duration);
            Assert.False(fixture.Service.IsRunning);
        }

        [Fact]
        public async Task IsRunningRemainsTrueUntilEveryConcurrentExecutionCompletes()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture(block: true);
            var first = fixture.Service.ExecuteAsync("first");
            var second = fixture.Service.ExecuteAsync("second");
            await fixture.Service.Entered.Task;
            Assert.True(fixture.Service.IsRunning);
            fixture.Service.ReleaseOne();
            await Task.WhenAny(first, second);
            Assert.True(fixture.Service.IsRunning);
            fixture.Service.ReleaseOne();
            await Task.WhenAll(first, second);
            Assert.False(fixture.Service.IsRunning);
        }

        [Fact]
        public async Task CancellationIsRecordedAndPropagated()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture(block: true);
            using var source = new CancellationTokenSource();
            var task = fixture.Service.ExecuteAsync("cancel", cancellationToken: source.Token);
            await fixture.Service.Entered.Task;
            source.Cancel();
            fixture.Service.ReleaseOne();
            await Assert.ThrowsAnyAsync<OperationCanceledException>(() => task);
            var records = fixture.Journal.GetByService(fixture.Service.Id);
            Assert.Single(records);
            Assert.Equal(WildsServiceExecutionOutcome.Cancelled, records[0].Outcome);
            Assert.False(fixture.Service.IsRunning);
        }

        [Fact]
        public async Task FailureIsRecordedWithoutReplacingOriginalException()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture(fail: true);
            var exception = await Assert.ThrowsAsync<InvalidOperationException>(() => fixture.Service.ExecuteAsync("fail"));
            Assert.Equal("controlled failure", exception.Message);
            var records = fixture.Journal.GetByService(fixture.Service.Id);
            Assert.Single(records);
            Assert.Equal(WildsServiceExecutionOutcome.Failed, records[0].Outcome);
            Assert.Contains("InvalidOperationException", records[0].FailureType);
            Assert.False(fixture.Service.IsRunning);
        }

        [Fact]
        public async Task ParentExecutionAndPulseArePreserved()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture();
            var parent = Guid.NewGuid();
            var pulse = Guid.NewGuid();
            var result = await fixture.Service.ExecuteAsync("child", parentExecutionId: parent, pulseId: pulse);
            Assert.True(fixture.Journal.TryGet(result.ExecutionId, out var record));
            Assert.Equal(parent, record!.ParentExecutionId);
            Assert.Equal(pulse, record.PulseId);
            Assert.Single(fixture.Journal.GetByPulse(pulse));
        }

        [Fact]
        public void CatalogRejectsDuplicateIdentityAndSlug()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture();
            var catalog = new WildsServiceCatalog();
            catalog.Register(fixture.Service);
            Assert.True(catalog.TryGet(fixture.Service.Id, out var byId));
            Assert.Same(fixture.Service, byId);
            Assert.True(catalog.TryGet(fixture.Service.Slug, out var bySlug));
            Assert.Same(fixture.Service, bySlug);
            Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => catalog.Register(fixture.Service));
        }

        private sealed class Fixture
        {
            public Fixture(bool block = false, bool fail = false)
            {
                Clock = new SystemWildsServiceClock();
                Journal = new WildsServiceExecutionJournal();
                var concreteFactory = new WildsServiceContextFactory(Clock);
                Service = new TestService(concreteFactory, Journal, Clock, block, fail);
            }
            public SystemWildsServiceClock Clock { get; }
            public WildsServiceExecutionJournal Journal { get; }
            public TestService Service { get; }
        }

        private sealed class TestService : WildsServiceBase<string, string>
        {
            private readonly bool _block;
            private readonly bool _fail;
            private readonly SemaphoreSlim _release = new SemaphoreSlim(0);
            private int _entered;
            public TestService(
                IWildsServiceContextFactory<IWildsServiceContext> contexts,
                IWildsServiceExecutionJournal journal,
                IWildsServiceClock clock,
                bool block,
                bool fail)
                : base("h1a-test-service", contexts, journal, clock)
            { _block = block; _fail = fail; }
            public TaskCompletionSource<bool> Entered { get; } =
                new TaskCompletionSource<bool>(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
            public void ReleaseOne() => _release.Release();
            protected override async Task<string> ExecuteCoreAsync(
                string request, IWildsServiceContext context, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
            {
                if (Interlocked.Increment(ref _entered) >= 2 || !_block) Entered.TrySetResult(true);
                else Entered.TrySetResult(true);
                if (_block) await _release.WaitAsync(cancellationToken);
                if (_fail) throw new InvalidOperationException("controlled failure");
                return request + "-result";
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\Entities\Certifications\E7H1A\Cert Tests\WildsServiceContextReadableTests.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Services;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services;
using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
using Xunit;

namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Certifications
{
    public sealed class WildsServiceContextReadableTests
    {
        [Fact]
        public async Task ServiceExecutionIsIdentifiableTimedAndQueryable()
        {
            TestLog.Section("E7-H1A - SERVICE CONTEXT PROVENANCE");
            var clock = new SystemWildsServiceClock();
            var journal = new WildsServiceExecutionJournal();
            var factory = new WildsServiceContextFactory(clock);
            var service = new EchoService(factory, journal, clock);
            var provenance = new object();
            var pulseId = Guid.NewGuid();
            var result = await service.ExecuteAsync("vectorize-this", provenance, pulseId: pulseId);
            Assert.True(journal.TryGet(result.ExecutionId, out var record));
            TestLog.State($"Service = {record!.ServiceSlug} ({record.ServiceId})");
            TestLog.State($"Execution = {record.Id}");
            TestLog.State($"Pulse correlation = {record.PulseId}");
            TestLog.State($"Outcome = {record.Outcome}");
            TestLog.State($"Started = {record.StartedAt:O}");
            TestLog.State($"Completed = {record.CompletedAt:O}");
            TestLog.State($"Duration = {record.Duration}");
            Assert.Equal(WildsServiceExecutionOutcome.Succeeded, record.Outcome);
            Assert.Same(provenance, record.Provenance);
            Assert.False(service.IsRunning);
            TestLog.Pass("The async service returned its execution identity and the journal preserved queryable temporal provenance.");
        }

        private sealed class EchoService : WildsServiceBase<string, string>
        {
            public EchoService(
                IWildsServiceContextFactory<IWildsServiceContext> contexts,
                IWildsServiceExecutionJournal journal,
                IWildsServiceClock clock)
                : base("readable-echo-service", contexts, journal, clock) { }
            protected override Task<string> ExecuteCoreAsync(
                string request, IWildsServiceContext context, CancellationToken cancellationToken) =>
                Task.FromResult(request + "-complete");
        }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\Entities\Certifications\E7H1B\Cert Tests\DiscoveryVectorizationServiceCertificationTests.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Fixtures;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Services;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Vectorization;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectorization;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectors;
using Xunit;

namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Certifications
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorizationServiceCertificationTests
    {
        [Fact]
        public async Task FactoryCreatedVectorizerExecutesServiceAndReturnsExecutionProvenance()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture();
            var entity = new TestEntity("h1b-owner");
            var discovery = CreateDiscovery(entity);
            var input = new DiscoveryVectorInput(discovery, new object());

            fixture.Service.Handler = request =>
                new DiscoveryVectorizationServiceResult(
                    new List<IDiscoveryVectorContribution>
                    {
                        new DiscoveryVectorContribution(request.Input, "trust", 0.5, 2.0),
                        new DiscoveryVectorContribution(request.Input, "trust", -0.25, 1.0),
                        new DiscoveryVectorContribution(request.Input, "risk", 0.75, 1.0)
                    });

            var vectorizer = new DiscoveryVectorizerFactory()
                .Create(fixture.Service, new AbsoluteMetric());

            var result = await vectorizer.VectorizeAsync(
                input,
                pulseId: Guid.NewGuid());

            Assert.NotEqual(Guid.Empty, result.ServiceExecutionId);
            Assert.True(fixture.Journal.TryGet(result.ServiceExecutionId, out var execution));
            Assert.Equal(WildsServiceExecutionOutcome.Succeeded, execution!.Outcome);
            Assert.Equal(2, result.Vector.Components.Count);
            Assert.Equal("risk", result.Vector.Components[0].Name);
            Assert.Equal(0.75, result.Vector.Components[0].Value);
            Assert.Equal("trust", result.Vector.Components[1].Name);
            Assert.Equal(0.75, result.Vector.Components[1].Value);
            Assert.Equal(3, result.Vector.Contributions.Count);
            Assert.Equal(1, fixture.Service.CallCount);
        }

        [Fact]
        public async Task ContributionOrderDoesNotChangeAggregatedGeometry()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture();
            var input = new DiscoveryVectorInput(CreateDiscovery(new TestEntity("order")));
            var first = new DiscoveryVectorContribution(input, "signal", 2.0, 0.5);
            var second = new DiscoveryVectorContribution(input, "signal", -1.0, 0.25);

            fixture.Service.Handler = _ =>
                new DiscoveryVectorizationServiceResult(
                    new List<IDiscoveryVectorContribution> { first, second });

            var one = await new DiscoveryVectorizerFactory()
                .Create(fixture.Service, new AbsoluteMetric())
                .VectorizeAsync(input);

            fixture.Service.Handler = _ =>
                new DiscoveryVectorizationServiceResult(
                    new List<IDiscoveryVectorContribution> { second, first });

            var two = await new DiscoveryVectorizerFactory()
                .Create(fixture.Service, new AbsoluteMetric())
                .VectorizeAsync(input);

            Assert.Equal(one.Vector.Components[0].Value, two.Vector.Components[0].Value);
        }

        [Fact]
        public async Task ForeignInputContributionFailsAfterServiceExecutionIsJournaled()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture();
            var requested = new DiscoveryVectorInput(CreateDiscovery(new TestEntity("requested")));
            var foreign = new DiscoveryVectorInput(CreateDiscovery(new TestEntity("foreign")));

            fixture.Service.Handler = _ =>
                new DiscoveryVectorizationServiceResult(
                    new List<IDiscoveryVectorContribution>
                    {
                        new DiscoveryVectorContribution(foreign, "signal", 1.0, 1.0)
                    });

            var vectorizer = new DiscoveryVectorizerFactory()
                .Create(fixture.Service, new AbsoluteMetric());

            await Assert.ThrowsAsync<InvalidOperationException>(
                () => vectorizer.VectorizeAsync(requested));

            var records = fixture.Journal.GetByService(fixture.Service.Id);
            Assert.Single(records);
            Assert.Equal(WildsServiceExecutionOutcome.Succeeded, records[0].Outcome);
        }

        [Fact]
        public async Task CancellationPropagatesThroughVectorizerAndServiceJournal()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture(block: true);
            var input = new DiscoveryVectorInput(CreateDiscovery(new TestEntity("cancel")));
            var vectorizer = new DiscoveryVectorizerFactory()
                .Create(fixture.Service, new AbsoluteMetric());

            using var source = new CancellationTokenSource();
            var task = vectorizer.VectorizeAsync(input, cancellationToken: source.Token);

            await fixture.Service.Entered.Task;
            source.Cancel();

            await Assert.ThrowsAnyAsync<OperationCanceledException>(() => task);

            var records = fixture.Journal.GetByService(fixture.Service.Id);
            Assert.Single(records);
            Assert.Equal(WildsServiceExecutionOutcome.Cancelled, records[0].Outcome);
            Assert.False(fixture.Service.IsRunning);
        }

        [Fact]
        public void FactoryRejectsMissingDependencies()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture();
            var factory = new DiscoveryVectorizerFactory();

            Assert.Throws<ArgumentNullException>(
                () => factory.Create(null!, new AbsoluteMetric()));

            Assert.Throws<ArgumentNullException>(
                () => factory.Create(fixture.Service, null!));
        }

        [Fact]
        public async Task VectorizationDoesNotResolveOrMutateSourceDiscovery()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture();
            var entity = new TestEntity("partial");
            var discovery = CreateDiscovery(entity);
            var input = new DiscoveryVectorInput(discovery);

            fixture.Service.Handler = request =>
                new DiscoveryVectorizationServiceResult(
                    new List<IDiscoveryVectorContribution>
                    {
                        new DiscoveryVectorContribution(
                            request.Input,
                            "support",
                            2.0 / 3.0,
                            1.0)
                    });

            var before = discovery.CurrentFactCount;

            var result = await new DiscoveryVectorizerFactory()
                .Create(fixture.Service, new AbsoluteMetric())
                .VectorizeAsync(input);

            Assert.Equal(before, discovery.CurrentFactCount);
            Assert.False(discovery.IsAvailable);
            Assert.Equal(2.0 / 3.0, result.Vector.Components[0].Value, 10);
        }

        private static TestDiscovery CreateDiscovery(IEntity entity) =>
            new TestDiscovery(
                entity,
                3,
                "H1B Discovery",
                Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));

        private sealed class AbsoluteMetric : IDiscoveryVectorMetric
        {
            public double GetMagnitude(IDiscoveryVectorPoint point)
            {
                var total = 0.0;
                for (var index = 0; index < point.Components.Count; index++)
                    total += Math.Abs(point.Components[index].Value);
                return total;
            }
        }

        private sealed class Fixture
        {
            public Fixture(bool block = false)
            {
                var clock = new SystemWildsServiceClock();
                Journal = new WildsServiceExecutionJournal();
                Service = new TestVectorizationService(
                    new WildsServiceContextFactory(clock),
                    Journal,
                    clock,
                    block);
            }

            public WildsServiceExecutionJournal Journal { get; }
            public TestVectorizationService Service { get; }
        }

        private sealed class TestVectorizationService : DiscoveryVectorizationServiceBase
        {
            private readonly bool _block;
            private readonly TaskCompletionSource<bool> _release =
                new TaskCompletionSource<bool>(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);

            public TestVectorizationService(
                IWildsServiceContextFactory<IWildsServiceContext> contexts,
                IWildsServiceExecutionJournal journal,
                IWildsServiceClock clock,
                bool block)
                : base("h1b-vectorization", contexts, journal, clock)
            {
                _block = block;
            }

            public Func<DiscoveryVectorizationRequest, DiscoveryVectorizationServiceResult>? Handler { get; set; }
            public int CallCount { get; private set; }
            public TaskCompletionSource<bool> Entered { get; } =
                new TaskCompletionSource<bool>(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);

            protected override async Task<DiscoveryVectorizationServiceResult> ExecuteCoreAsync(
                DiscoveryVectorizationRequest request,
                IWildsServiceContext context,
                CancellationToken cancellationToken)
            {
                CallCount++;
                Entered.TrySetResult(true);

                if (_block)
                {
                    var cancellation = new TaskCompletionSource<bool>(
                        TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);

                    using (cancellationToken.Register(() => cancellation.TrySetCanceled(cancellationToken)))
                    {
                        await Task.WhenAny(_release.Task, cancellation.Task);
                        cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                    }
                }

                return Handler?.Invoke(request) ??
                    new DiscoveryVectorizationServiceResult(
                        new List<IDiscoveryVectorContribution>
                        {
                            new DiscoveryVectorContribution(request.Input, "default", 1.0, 1.0)
                        });
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\Entities\Certifications\E7H1B\Cert Tests\DiscoveryVectorizationServiceReadableTests.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Fixtures;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Services;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Vectorization;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectorization;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectors;
using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
using Xunit;

namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Certifications
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorizationServiceReadableTests
    {
        [Fact]
        public async Task AsyncConsumerServiceTurnsKnowledgeIntoAttributedGeometry()
        {
            TestLog.Section("E7-H1B - ASYNC DISCOVERY VECTORIZATION");

            var clock = new SystemWildsServiceClock();
            var journal = new WildsServiceExecutionJournal();
            var service = new ReadableService(
                new WildsServiceContextFactory(clock),
                journal,
                clock);

            var entity = new TestEntity("soup-gobbo");
            var discovery = new TestDiscovery(
                entity,
                3,
                "Soup Risk",
                "soup-risk-h1b");

            var input = new DiscoveryVectorInput(
                discovery,
                "consumer-authorized-sample");

            var pulseId = Guid.NewGuid();

            var result = await new DiscoveryVectorizerFactory()
                .Create(service, new AbsoluteMetric())
                .VectorizeAsync(
                    input,
                    provenance: "H1B readable",
                    pulseId: pulseId);

            Assert.True(journal.TryGet(result.ServiceExecutionId, out var execution));

            TestLog.State($"Service = {execution!.ServiceSlug}");
            TestLog.State($"Execution = {result.ServiceExecutionId}");
            TestLog.State($"Pulse = {execution.PulseId}");
            TestLog.State($"Discovery resolved = {discovery.IsAvailable}");
            TestLog.State($"Risk = {result.Vector.Components[0].Value:R}");
            TestLog.State($"Trust = {result.Vector.Components[1].Value:R}");
            TestLog.State($"Magnitude = {result.Vector.Magnitude:R}");

            Assert.False(discovery.IsAvailable);
            Assert.Equal(WildsServiceExecutionOutcome.Succeeded, execution.Outcome);
            Assert.Equal(2, result.Vector.Components.Count);
            Assert.Equal("risk", result.Vector.Components[0].Name);
            Assert.Equal(0.80, result.Vector.Components[0].Value, 10);
            Assert.Equal("trust", result.Vector.Components[1].Name);
            Assert.Equal(0.40, result.Vector.Components[1].Value, 10);
            Assert.Equal(1.20, result.Vector.Magnitude, 10);

            TestLog.Pass(
                "The consumer-provided async service produced exact contributions; Discoveries validated and aggregated them into attributable geometry without resolving the source Discovery.");
        }

        private sealed class ReadableService : DiscoveryVectorizationServiceBase
        {
            public ReadableService(
                IWildsServiceContextFactory<IWildsServiceContext> contexts,
                IWildsServiceExecutionJournal journal,
                IWildsServiceClock clock)
                : base("soup-risk-vectorization", contexts, journal, clock)
            {
            }

            protected override Task<DiscoveryVectorizationServiceResult> ExecuteCoreAsync(
                DiscoveryVectorizationRequest request,
                IWildsServiceContext context,
                CancellationToken cancellationToken)
            {
                return Task.FromResult(
                    new DiscoveryVectorizationServiceResult(
                        new List<IDiscoveryVectorContribution>
                        {
                            new DiscoveryVectorContribution(request.Input, "risk", 0.65, 1.0),
                            new DiscoveryVectorContribution(request.Input, "risk", 0.15, 1.0),
                            new DiscoveryVectorContribution(request.Input, "trust", 0.40, 1.0)
                        }));
            }
        }

        private sealed class AbsoluteMetric : IDiscoveryVectorMetric
        {
            public double GetMagnitude(IDiscoveryVectorPoint point)
            {
                var total = 0.0;
                for (var index = 0; index < point.Components.Count; index++)
                    total += Math.Abs(point.Components[index].Value);
                return total;
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\Entities\Certifications\E7H2\DiscoveryVectorMomentumCurveCertificationTests.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Motion;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Curves;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Motion;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectors;
using Xunit;

namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Certifications
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorMomentumCurveCertificationTests
    {
        private static IDiscoveryVectorMotionResult Raw(double v,double a)
        { var f=new H2TestFixture(); var e=f.Eval(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,("risk",.5)); return new DiscoveryVectorMotionResult(Guid.NewGuid(),new DiscoveryVectorMotionServiceResult(e,new List<IDiscoveryVectorMotion>{new DiscoveryVectorMotion("risk",v,a)})); }
        [Fact] public void IdentityCurvePreservesRawMotion()
        { var raw=Raw(.6,-.2); var r=new DiscoveryVectorMomentumProjector().Project(raw,new IdentityDiscoveryMotionCurve()); Assert.Equal(.6,r.AdjustedMotion[0].Velocity); Assert.Equal(-.2,r.AdjustedMotion[0].Acceleration); Assert.Same(raw.Motion[0],r.RawMotion[0]); }
        [Fact] public void BoundedCurveIsDeterministicAndPreservesSignsAndZero()
        { var curve=new BoundedTanhDiscoveryMotionCurve(2,1); var p=new DiscoveryVectorMomentumProjector(); var a=p.Project(Raw(.8,-.5),curve); var b=p.Project(Raw(.8,-.5),curve); Assert.Equal(a.AdjustedMotion[0].Velocity,b.AdjustedMotion[0].Velocity); Assert.True(a.AdjustedMotion[0].Velocity>0); Assert.True(a.AdjustedMotion[0].Acceleration<0); Assert.Equal(0,curve.TransformVelocity("risk",0)); }
        [Fact] public void InvalidCurveParametersFail()
        { Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(()=>new BoundedTanhDiscoveryMotionCurve(0,1)); Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(()=>new BoundedTanhDiscoveryMotionCurve(1,0)); }
        [Fact] public void DifferentCurvesProduceDifferentAdjustedMotionWithoutChangingRaw()
        { var raw=Raw(2,1); var p=new DiscoveryVectorMomentumProjector(); var identity=p.Project(raw,new IdentityDiscoveryMotionCurve()); var bounded=p.Project(raw,new BoundedTanhDiscoveryMotionCurve(1,1)); Assert.NotEqual(identity.AdjustedMotion[0].Velocity,bounded.AdjustedMotion[0].Velocity); Assert.Equal(2,raw.Motion[0].Velocity); Assert.Equal(raw.ServiceExecutionId,bounded.MotionServiceExecutionId); }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\Entities\Certifications\E7H2\DiscoveryVectorMotionReadableTests.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Curves;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Motion;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services;
using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
using Xunit;

namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Certifications
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorMotionReadableTests
    {
        [Fact] public async Task KnowledgePositionBecomesAttributedTrajectory()
        {
            TestLog.Section("E7-H2 - KNOWLEDGE TRAJECTORY");
            var f=new H2TestFixture(); var t=DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
            var e0=f.Eval(t,("risk",.2),("trust",.7)); var e1=f.Eval(t.AddSeconds(1),("risk",.5),("trust",.55)); var e2=f.Eval(t.AddSeconds(2),("risk",.8),("trust",.4));
            var series=f.Series(e0,e1,e2); var clock=new SystemWildsServiceClock(); var journal=new WildsServiceExecutionJournal(); var service=new FiniteDifferenceDiscoveryVectorMotionService("knowledge-motion",new WildsServiceContextFactory(clock),journal,clock);
            var raw=await new DiscoveryVectorMotionEvaluatorFactory().Create(service).EvaluateAsync(series,pulseId:Guid.NewGuid());
            var momentum=new DiscoveryVectorMomentumProjector().Project(raw,new BoundedTanhDiscoveryMotionCurve(2,1));
            TestLog.State($"Scope = {series.Scope}"); TestLog.State($"Vectorization executions = {string.Join(", ",series.Evaluations.Select(x=>x.VectorizationExecutionId))}"); TestLog.State($"Motion execution = {raw.ServiceExecutionId}");
            for(var i=0;i<raw.Motion.Count;i++){ TestLog.State($"{raw.Motion[i].Dimension}: velocity={raw.Motion[i].Velocity:R}, acceleration={raw.Motion[i].Acceleration:R}, adjustedVelocity={momentum.AdjustedMotion[i].Velocity:R}"); }
            Assert.Equal(.3,raw.Motion.Single(x=>x.Dimension=="risk").Velocity,10); Assert.Equal(-.15,raw.Motion.Single(x=>x.Dimension=="trust").Velocity,10); Assert.False(f.Discovery.IsAvailable);
            TestLog.Pass("Immutable vector history produced attributable raw motion and explicit curve-adjusted momentum without changing Discovery resolution.");
        }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\Entities\Certifications\E7H2\DiscoveryVectorMotionServiceCertificationTests.cs

using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Motion;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Motion;
using Xunit;

namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Certifications
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorMotionServiceCertificationTests
    {
        private static (IDiscoveryVectorMotionEvaluator Evaluator, WildsServiceExecutionJournal Journal) Create()
        { var clock=new SystemWildsServiceClock(); var journal=new WildsServiceExecutionJournal(); var service=new FiniteDifferenceDiscoveryVectorMotionService("h2-motion",new WildsServiceContextFactory(clock),journal,clock); return(new DiscoveryVectorMotionEvaluatorFactory().Create(service),journal); }
        [Fact] public async Task FirstObservationProducesNoMotion()
        { var f=new H2TestFixture(); var x=Create(); var r=await x.Evaluator.EvaluateAsync(f.Series(f.Eval(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,("risk",.2)))); Assert.Empty(r.Motion); }
        [Fact] public async Task SecondObservationProducesVelocityAndZeroAcceleration()
        { var f=new H2TestFixture(); var t=DateTimeOffset.UtcNow; var x=Create(); var r=await x.Evaluator.EvaluateAsync(f.Series(f.Eval(t,("risk",.2)),f.Eval(t.AddSeconds(2),("risk",.8)))); Assert.Equal(.3,r.Motion[0].Velocity,10); Assert.Equal(0,r.Motion[0].Acceleration,10); }
        [Fact] public async Task ThirdObservationDerivesAccelerationAndReversal()
        { var f=new H2TestFixture(); var t=DateTimeOffset.UtcNow; var x=Create(); var r=await x.Evaluator.EvaluateAsync(f.Series(f.Eval(t,("risk",.2)),f.Eval(t.AddSeconds(1),("risk",.8)),f.Eval(t.AddSeconds(2),("risk",.4)))); Assert.Equal(-.4,r.Motion[0].Velocity,10); Assert.Equal(-1.0,r.Motion[0].Acceleration,10); }
        [Fact] public async Task MissingDimensionsAreOmittedAndExecutionIsJournaled()
        { var f=new H2TestFixture(); var t=DateTimeOffset.UtcNow; var x=Create(); var pulse=Guid.NewGuid(); var r=await x.Evaluator.EvaluateAsync(f.Series(f.Eval(t,("risk",.2),("trust",.7)),f.Eval(t.AddSeconds(1),("risk",.5))),pulseId:pulse); Assert.Single(r.Motion); Assert.True(x.Journal.TryGet(r.ServiceExecutionId,out var record)); Assert.Equal(pulse,record!.PulseId); }
        [Fact] public async Task SourceVectorsAndResolutionRemainUntouched()
        { var f=new H2TestFixture(); var t=DateTimeOffset.UtcNow; var a=f.Eval(t,("risk",.2)); var b=f.Eval(t.AddSeconds(1),("risk",.5)); var before=f.Discovery.CurrentFactCount; var x=Create(); await x.Evaluator.EvaluateAsync(f.Series(a,b)); Assert.Equal(.2,a.Vector.Components[0].Value); Assert.Equal(.5,b.Vector.Components[0].Value); Assert.Equal(before,f.Discovery.CurrentFactCount); Assert.False(f.Discovery.IsAvailable); }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\Entities\Certifications\E7H2\DiscoveryVectorSeriesCertificationTests.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Motion;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Motion;
using Xunit;

namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Certifications
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorSeriesCertificationTests
    {
        [Fact]
        public void SeriesPreservesOrderCurrentPreviousAndExecutionIds()
        {
            var fixture = new H2TestFixture();
            var timestamp = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
            var first = fixture.Eval(timestamp, ("risk", 0.2));
            var second = fixture.Eval(timestamp.AddSeconds(1), ("risk", 0.5));

            var series = fixture.Series(first, second);

            Assert.Same(second, series.Current);
            Assert.Same(first, series.Previous);
            Assert.Equal(
                first.VectorizationExecutionId,
                series.Evaluations[0].VectorizationExecutionId);
        }

        [Fact]
        public void SeriesSnapshotsCallerCollections()
        {
            var fixture = new H2TestFixture();
            var evaluation = fixture.Eval(
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
                ("risk", 0.2));

            var inputs =
                new List<IDiscoveryVectorInput>
                {
                    fixture.Input
                };

            var evaluations =
                new List<IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation>
                {
                    evaluation
                };

            var series =
                new DiscoveryVectorEvaluationSeriesFactory().Create(
                    "scope",
                    inputs,
                    evaluations);

            inputs.Clear();
            evaluations.Clear();

            Assert.Single(series.Inputs);
            Assert.Single(series.Evaluations);
        }

        [Fact]
        public void EqualOrDescendingTimesFail()
        {
            var fixture = new H2TestFixture();
            var timestamp = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
            var first = fixture.Eval(timestamp, ("risk", 0.2));
            var equal = fixture.Eval(timestamp, ("risk", 0.5));
            var descending = fixture.Eval(
                timestamp.AddSeconds(-1),
                ("risk", 0.8));

            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(
                () => fixture.Series(first, equal));

            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(
                () => fixture.Series(first, descending));
        }

        [Fact]
        public void SingleEvaluationHasCurrentWithoutPrevious()
        {
            var fixture = new H2TestFixture();
            var evaluation = fixture.Eval(
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
                ("risk", 0.2));

            var series = fixture.Series(evaluation);

            Assert.Same(evaluation, series.Current);
            Assert.Null(series.Previous);
        }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\Entities\Certifications\E7H2\H2TestFixture.cs

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Fixtures;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Motion;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Motion;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectors;

namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Certifications
{
    internal sealed class H2TestFixture
    {
        public H2TestFixture()
        {
            Entity = new TestEntity("h2-owner");
            Discovery = new TestDiscovery(Entity, 3, "H2 Discovery", "h2-discovery");
            Input = new DiscoveryVectorInput(Discovery, "h2-input");
        }
        public TestEntity Entity { get; }
        public TestDiscovery Discovery { get; }
        public DiscoveryVectorInput Input { get; }
        public IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation Eval(DateTimeOffset at, params (string,double)[] values)
        {
            var components = new List<IDiscoveryVectorComponent>();
            var contributions = new List<IDiscoveryVectorContribution>();
            foreach (var value in values)
            {
                components.Add(new DiscoveryVectorComponent(value.Item1, value.Item2));
                contributions.Add(new DiscoveryVectorContribution(Input, value.Item1, value.Item2, 1.0));
            }
            var vector = new DiscoveryVector(components, contributions, new List<IDiscoveryVectorMotion>(), new AbsoluteMetric(), at);
            return new DiscoveryVectorEvaluation(vector, Guid.NewGuid(), at);
        }
        public IDiscoveryVectorEvaluationSeries Series(params IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation[] evaluations) =>
            new DiscoveryVectorEvaluationSeriesFactory().Create("h2-scope", new List<IDiscoveryVectorInput>{Input}, evaluations);
        private sealed class AbsoluteMetric : IDiscoveryVectorMetric
        {
            public double GetMagnitude(IDiscoveryVectorPoint point)
            { var total=0.0; for(var i=0;i<point.Components.Count;i++) total += Math.Abs(point.Components[i].Value); return total; }
        }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\Entities\Certifications\E7H3\DiscoveryClusterMappingCertificationTests.cs

using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Clusters;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Services;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Clusters;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Registries;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services;
using Xunit;

namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Certifications
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryClusterMappingCertificationTests
    {
        [Fact]
        public async Task ExampleDocumentImportsAtomicallyAndIsQueryable()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture();
            var content = ReadExample();

            var result = await fixture.Service.ExecuteAsync(
                new DiscoveryClusterMappingImportRequest(
                    "industrial-condition-regions.json",
                    content),
                pulseId: Guid.NewGuid());

            Assert.Equal(2, result.Value.ImportedCount);
            Assert.Equal(2, fixture.Registry.Mappings.Count);

            Assert.True(
                fixture.Registry.TryGetBySemanticKey(
                    "MaintenanceConcern",
                    out var mapping));

            Assert.NotNull(mapping);
            Assert.Equal("maintenance-concern", mapping!.Slug);
            Assert.Equal(0.70, mapping.Centroid["mechanical-stress"]);
            Assert.Equal(0.18, mapping.Radius);

            Assert.True(
                fixture.Journal.TryGet(
                    result.ExecutionId,
                    out var execution));

            Assert.NotNull(execution);
            Assert.Equal(
                WildsServiceExecutionOutcome.Succeeded,
                execution!.Outcome);
        }

        [Fact]
        public async Task DuplicateImportFailsWithoutPartialMutation()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture();

            var request =
                new DiscoveryClusterMappingImportRequest(
                    "example.json",
                    ReadExample());

            await fixture.Service.ExecuteAsync(request);

            await Assert.ThrowsAsync<InvalidOperationException>(
                () => fixture.Service.ExecuteAsync(request));

            Assert.Equal(2, fixture.Registry.Mappings.Count);
        }

        [Fact]
        public async Task InvalidDocumentFailsWithoutRegistryMutation()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture();

            var invalid =
                ReadExample().Replace(
                    "\"radius\": 0.18",
                    "\"radius\": -0.18");

            await Assert.ThrowsAsync<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(
                () => fixture.Service.ExecuteAsync(
                    new DiscoveryClusterMappingImportRequest(
                        "invalid.json",
                        invalid)));

            Assert.Empty(fixture.Registry.Mappings);
        }

        [Fact]
        public async Task ImportedMeaningRemainsConsumerOwnedOpaqueMetadata()
        {
            var fixture = new Fixture();

            await fixture.Service.ExecuteAsync(
                new DiscoveryClusterMappingImportRequest(
                    "example.json",
                    ReadExample()));

            Assert.True(
                fixture.Registry.TryGet(
                    "maintenance-concern",
                    out var mapping));

            Assert.NotNull(mapping);
            Assert.Equal(
                "MaintenanceConcern",
                mapping!.SemanticKey);

            Assert.Equal(
                "Maintenance Concern",
                mapping.Metadata["displayName"]);

            Assert.Equal(
                "hypersphere",
                mapping.GeometryType);
        }

        private static string ReadExample()
        {
            var path =
                Path.GetFullPath(
                    Path.Combine(
                        AppContext.BaseDirectory,
                        "..",
                        "..",
                        "..",
                        "Entities",
                        "Certifications",
                        "E7H3",
                        "Examples",
                        "industrial-condition-regions.json"));

            if (!File.Exists(path))
            {
                throw new FileNotFoundException(
                    "The E7-H3 cluster mapping certification example was not found.",
                    path);
            }

            return File.ReadAllText(path);
        }

        private sealed class Fixture
        {
            public Fixture()
            {
                var clock = new SystemWildsServiceClock();

                Registry =
                    new DiscoveryClusterMappingRegistry();

                Journal =
                    new WildsServiceExecutionJournal();

                Service =
                    new DiscoveryClusterMappingImportService(
                        "cluster-mapping-import",
                        Registry,
                        new WildsServiceContextFactory(clock),
                        Journal,
                        clock);
            }

            public DiscoveryClusterMappingRegistry Registry { get; }

            public WildsServiceExecutionJournal Journal { get; }

            public DiscoveryClusterMappingImportService Service { get; }
        }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\Entities\Certifications\E7H3\DiscoveryClusterMappingReadableTests.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Clusters;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Clusters;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Registries;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services;
using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
using Xunit;

namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Certifications
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryClusterMappingReadableTests
    {
        [Fact]
        public async Task UserMappingDocumentBecomesQueryableClusterRegistryData()
        {
            TestLog.Section("E7-H3A - USER CLUSTER MAPPING IMPORT");
            var clock = new SystemWildsServiceClock();
            var registry = new DiscoveryClusterMappingRegistry();
            var journal = new WildsServiceExecutionJournal();
            var service = new DiscoveryClusterMappingImportService(
                "cluster-mapping-import", registry,
                new WildsServiceContextFactory(clock), journal, clock);
            var path =
                Path.GetFullPath(
                    Path.Combine(
                        AppContext.BaseDirectory,
                        "..",
                        "..",
                        "..",
                        "Entities",
                        "Certifications",
                        "E7H3",
                        "Examples",
                        "industrial-condition-regions.json"));

            if (!File.Exists(path))
            {
                throw new FileNotFoundException(
                    "The E7-H3 cluster mapping readable example was not found.",
                    path);
            }
            var result = await service.ExecuteAsync(
                new DiscoveryClusterMappingImportRequest(path, File.ReadAllText(path)),
                provenance: "consumer supplied mapping document");
            Assert.True(registry.TryGetBySemanticKey("MaintenanceConcern", out var mapping));
            TestLog.State($"Document = {result.Value.DocumentSlug} ({result.Value.DocumentId})");
            TestLog.State($"Imported mappings = {result.Value.ImportedCount}");
            TestLog.State($"Execution = {result.ExecutionId}");
            TestLog.State($"Semantic key = {mapping!.SemanticKey}");
            TestLog.State($"Geometry = {mapping.GeometryType}; radius={mapping.Radius:R}");
            TestLog.State($"Centroid dimensions = {mapping.Centroid.Count}");
            Assert.Equal("Maintenance Concern", mapping.Metadata["displayName"]);
            TestLog.Pass("The consumer mapping document was validated by an attributable async service and stored atomically in the cluster mapping registry.");
        }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\Entities\Certifications\E7H3\DiscoveryClusterRelationshipCertificationTests.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Clusters;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Motion;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Clusters;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Motion;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectors;
using Xunit;

namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Certifications
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryClusterRelationshipCertificationTests
    {
        [Fact] public void LocateReturnsCentroidAndSignedBoundaryDistance()
        {
            var f=new H2TestFixture();var e=f.Eval(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,("x",3.0),("y",4.0));var m=Mapping(0,0,2);
            var r=new HypersphereDiscoveryClusterRelationshipProjector().Locate(e,m);
            Assert.Equal(5,r.DistanceToCentroid,10);Assert.Equal(3,r.SignedDistanceToBoundary,10);Assert.False(r.IsInside);Assert.Equal(1.2,r.NearestBoundaryPoint["x"],10);Assert.Equal(1.6,r.NearestBoundaryPoint["y"],10);
        }
        [Fact] public void InsideProducesNegativeBoundaryDistance()
        { var f=new H2TestFixture();var r=new HypersphereDiscoveryClusterRelationshipProjector().Locate(f.Eval(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,("x",.5),("y",0)),Mapping(0,0,1));Assert.True(r.IsInside);Assert.Equal(-.5,r.SignedDistanceToBoundary,10); }
        [Fact] public void MissingMappedDimensionFails()
        { var f=new H2TestFixture();Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(()=>new HypersphereDiscoveryClusterRelationshipProjector().Locate(f.Eval(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,("x",.5)),Mapping(0,0,1))); }
        internal static IDiscoveryClusterMapping Mapping(double x,double y,double radius)=>new DiscoveryClusterMapping(Guid.NewGuid(),"target",Guid.NewGuid(),"doc","1","Target","hypersphere",new Dictionary<string,double>{{"x",x},{"y",y}},radius);
        internal static IDiscoveryVectorMotionResult Motion(IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation e,double x,double y)=>new DiscoveryVectorMotionResult(Guid.NewGuid(),new DiscoveryVectorMotionServiceResult(e,new List<IDiscoveryVectorMotion>{new DiscoveryVectorMotion("x",x,0),new DiscoveryVectorMotion("y",y,0)}));
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\Entities\Certifications\E7H3\DiscoveryClusterRelationshipReadableTests.cs
################################################################
using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Clusters;
using WildsEngine.Tests.Logging;
using Xunit;

namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Certifications
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryClusterRelationshipReadableTests
    {
        [Fact] public void SnapshotTrajectoryReturnsSpatialAndTemporalClusterRelationship()
        {
            TestLog.Section("E7-H3B/C/D - CLUSTER RELATIONSHIP AND OCCUPANCY");
            var fixture=new H2TestFixture();var evaluation=fixture.Eval(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,("x",-3.0),("y",0.0));
            var projection=new HypersphereDiscoveryClusterRelationshipProjector().Project(DiscoveryClusterRelationshipCertificationTests.Motion(evaluation,1.0,0.0),DiscoveryClusterRelationshipCertificationTests.Mapping(0,0,1));
            TestLog.State($"Semantic key = {projection.Spatial.Mapping.SemanticKey}");
            TestLog.State($"Centroid distance = {projection.Spatial.DistanceToCentroid:R}");
            TestLog.State($"Signed boundary distance = {projection.Spatial.SignedDistanceToBoundary:R}");
            TestLog.State($"Transit = {projection.TransitKind}");
            TestLog.State($"Entry in = {projection.TimeToEntrySeconds:R} seconds");
            TestLog.State($"Exit in = {projection.TimeToExitSeconds:R} seconds");
            TestLog.State($"Projected duration inside = {projection.ProjectedDurationInsideSeconds:R} seconds");
            Assert.Equal(2,projection.TimeToEntrySeconds!.Value,10);Assert.Equal(2,projection.ProjectedDurationInsideSeconds!.Value,10);
            TestLog.Pass("The current snapshot was located against consumer geometry and its constant-velocity trajectory produced attributable entry, exit, and projected occupancy duration.");
        }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Core.Tests\Entities\Certifications\E7H3\DiscoveryClusterTrajectoryCertificationTests.cs
################################################################
using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Clusters;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Clusters;
using Xunit;

namespace WildsEngine.Core.Tests.Entities.Certifications
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryClusterTrajectoryCertificationTests
    {
        [Fact] public void OutsideTrajectoryCrossesSphereAndReturnsEntryExitDuration()
        { var f=new H2TestFixture();var e=f.Eval(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,("x",-3.0),("y",0));var r=new HypersphereDiscoveryClusterRelationshipProjector().Project(DiscoveryClusterRelationshipCertificationTests.Motion(e,1,0),DiscoveryClusterRelationshipCertificationTests.Mapping(0,0,1));Assert.Equal(DiscoveryClusterTransitKind.PassingThrough,r.TransitKind);Assert.Equal(2,r.TimeToEntrySeconds!.Value,10);Assert.Equal(4,r.TimeToExitSeconds!.Value,10);Assert.Equal(2,r.ProjectedDurationInsideSeconds!.Value,10);Assert.Equal(-1,r.EntryPoint!["x"],10);Assert.Equal(1,r.ExitPoint!["x"],10); }
        [Fact] public void FlybyReturnsClosestApproachWithoutOccupancy()
        { var f=new H2TestFixture();var e=f.Eval(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,("x",-3.0),("y",2.0));var r=new HypersphereDiscoveryClusterRelationshipProjector().Project(DiscoveryClusterRelationshipCertificationTests.Motion(e,1,0),DiscoveryClusterRelationshipCertificationTests.Mapping(0,0,1));Assert.Null(r.TimeToEntrySeconds);Assert.Null(r.ProjectedDurationInsideSeconds);Assert.Equal(3,r.TimeToClosestApproachSeconds,10);Assert.Equal(2,r.ClosestApproachDistanceToCentroid,10);Assert.Equal(1,r.ClosestApproachDistanceToBoundary,10); }
        [Fact] public void InsideTrajectoryReturnsExitAndRemainingDuration()
        { var f=new H2TestFixture();var e=f.Eval(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,("x",0.0),("y",0));var r=new HypersphereDiscoveryClusterRelationshipProjector().Project(DiscoveryClusterRelationshipCertificationTests.Motion(e,1,0),DiscoveryClusterRelationshipCertificationTests.Mapping(0,0,1));Assert.Equal(DiscoveryClusterTransitKind.Exiting,r.TransitKind);Assert.Equal(0,r.TimeToEntrySeconds!.Value);Assert.Equal(1,r.TimeToExitSeconds!.Value,10);Assert.Equal(1,r.ProjectedDurationInsideSeconds!.Value,10); }
        [Fact] public void RecedingTrajectoryDoesNotInventFutureIntersection()
        { var f=new H2TestFixture();var e=f.Eval(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,("x",2.0),("y",0));var r=new HypersphereDiscoveryClusterRelationshipProjector().Project(DiscoveryClusterRelationshipCertificationTests.Motion(e,1,0),DiscoveryClusterRelationshipCertificationTests.Mapping(0,0,1));Assert.True(r.IsReceding);Assert.Null(r.TimeToEntrySeconds);Assert.Equal(0,r.TimeToClosestApproachSeconds); }
        [Fact] public void ProjectionExplicitlyDeclaresConstantVelocityBasis()
        { var f=new H2TestFixture();var e=f.Eval(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,("x",-3.0),("y",0));var r=new HypersphereDiscoveryClusterRelationshipProjector().Project(DiscoveryClusterRelationshipCertificationTests.Motion(e,1,0),DiscoveryClusterRelationshipCertificationTests.Mapping(0,0,1));Assert.Equal("constant-velocity",r.ProjectionBasis); }
    }
}

Set-StrictMode -Version Latest

$RunnersRoot = $PSScriptRoot
$EntitiesRoot = Split-Path -Parent $RunnersRoot
$TestsRoot = Split-Path -Parent $EntitiesRoot
$RepoRoot = Split-Path -Parent $TestsRoot
$Project = Join-Path $TestsRoot "WildsEngine.Core.Tests.csproj"
$ReportDir = Join-Path $EntitiesRoot "Reports"
$Timestamp = Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd-HHmmss"
$Report = Join-Path $ReportDir "E7-H0-Certification-$Timestamp.txt"
$Latest = Join-Path $ReportDir "E7-H0-Certification-Latest.txt"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ReportDir -Force | Out-Null

function Write-Line([string]$Text = "") {
    Write-Host $Text
    $Text | Out-File -LiteralPath $Report -Append -Encoding utf8
}
function Invoke-Logged([string]$Title, [scriptblock]$Command) {
    Write-Line ""
    Write-Line "============================================================"
    Write-Line $Title
    Write-Line "============================================================"
    & $Command 2>&1 | ForEach-Object { Write-Line ([string]$_) }
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "$Title failed with exit code $LASTEXITCODE." }
}

Write-Line "============================================================"
Write-Line "WILDS DISCOVERIES E7-H0 CERTIFICATION"
Write-Line "============================================================"
Write-Line "Gate       : E7-H0"
Write-Line "Title      : Geometry Foundation"
Write-Line "Project    : $Project"
Write-Line "Started    : $(Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss zzz')"

$core = @(git -C $RepoRoot status --short -- .\WildsEngine.Core)
if ($core.Count -gt 0) { $core | ForEach-Object { Write-Line $_ }; throw "Core has changes. H0 will not proceed." }
Write-Line "Core guard : PASS"

Invoke-Logged "E7-H0 RELEASE BUILD" {
    dotnet build $Project -c Release
}
Invoke-Logged "E7-H0 ASSERTION TESTS" {
    dotnet test $Project -c Release --no-build --filter "FullyQualifiedName~EntityDiscoveryGeometryFoundationCertificationTests"
}
Invoke-Logged "E7-H0 READABLE TESTS" {
    dotnet test $Project -c Release --no-build --filter "FullyQualifiedName~EntityDiscoveryGeometryFoundationReadableTests" --logger "console;verbosity=detailed"
}

$coreAfter = @(git -C $RepoRoot status --short -- .\WildsEngine.Core)
if ($coreAfter.Count -gt 0) { $coreAfter | ForEach-Object { Write-Line $_ }; throw "Core changed during H0." }
Write-Line ""
Write-Line "RESULT: PASS"
Write-Line "Core unchanged. Geometry foundation certified."
Copy-Item -LiteralPath $Report -Destination $Latest -Force
Write-Host "Report : $Report"
Write-Host "Latest : $Latest"

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\Clusters\DiscoveryClusterMappingImportRequest.cs
################################################################
using System;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Clusters
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryClusterMappingImportRequest
    {
        public DiscoveryClusterMappingImportRequest(string sourceName, string documentContent)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sourceName))
                throw new ArgumentException("Source name is required.", nameof(sourceName));
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(documentContent))
                throw new ArgumentException("Mapping document content is required.", nameof(documentContent));
            SourceName = sourceName.Trim();
            DocumentContent = documentContent;
        }
        public string SourceName { get; }
        public string DocumentContent { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\Clusters\DiscoveryClusterMappingImportResult.cs
################################################################
using System;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Clusters
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryClusterMappingImportResult
    {
        public DiscoveryClusterMappingImportResult(Guid documentId, string documentSlug, int importedCount)
        {
            if (documentId == Guid.Empty) throw new ArgumentException("Document ID is required.", nameof(documentId));
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(documentSlug)) throw new ArgumentException("Document slug is required.", nameof(documentSlug));
            if (importedCount <= 0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(importedCount));
            DocumentId = documentId;
            DocumentSlug = documentSlug;
            ImportedCount = importedCount;
        }
        public Guid DocumentId { get; }
        public string DocumentSlug { get; }
        public int ImportedCount { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\Clusters\DiscoveryClusterTransitKind.cs
################################################################
namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Clusters
{
    public enum DiscoveryClusterTransitKind
    {
        Stationary = 0,
        Approaching = 1,
        Receding = 2,
        Entering = 3,
        Exiting = 4,
        PassingThrough = 5,
        RemainingInside = 6,
        Flyby = 7
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\IConsequence.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Represents a consumer-defined projected outcome derived
    /// from one or more scoped Truths.
    ///
    /// A Consequence describes what may result. It does not
    /// prescribe behavior.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IConsequence :
        IIdentifier
    {
        string Name { get; }

        object? Value { get; }

        IReadOnlyList<ITruth> Truths { get; }

        double Magnitude { get; }

        double Confidence { get; }

        DateTimeOffset ProjectedAt { get; }
    }
}

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions

    /// <summary>
    /// Consumer-supplied policy that projects possible
    /// Consequences from scoped Truths.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IConsequenceProjectionPolicy
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IConsequence> Project(
            IReadOnlyList<ITruth> truths);
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\IDiscoveryCluster.cs
################################################################
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Represents one geometrically coherent region found within
    /// a Discovery-vector sample.
    ///
    /// A cluster is a derived result and has no independent
    /// domain or Wilds runtime identity.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IDiscoveryCluster
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVector> Members { get; }

        IDiscoveryVectorPoint Centroid { get; }

        double Density { get; }

        double Dispersion { get; }

        double Coherence { get; }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Finds geometric structure within immutable Discovery
    /// vectors.
    ///
    /// This is knowledge-space clustering, not Core topology
    /// clustering.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IDiscoveryClusterer
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryCluster> Cluster(
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVector> vectors);
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\IDiscoveryVector.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Represents one immutable, provenance-bearing evaluation
    /// of Discovery geometry.
    ///
    /// A Discovery vector is not independently identifiable.
    /// It is an evaluation result over authoritative sources.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IDiscoveryVector :
        IDiscoveryVectorPoint
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorContribution> Contributions { get; }

        IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorMotion> Motion { get; }

        IDiscoveryVectorMetric Metric { get; }

        double Magnitude { get; }

        DateTimeOffset EvaluatedAt { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\IDiscoveryVectorComponent.cs
################################################################
namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Represents one consumer-defined coordinate in Discovery
    /// geometry.
    ///
    /// Wilds owns the numeric mechanics. The consumer owns the
    /// meaning of Name.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IDiscoveryVectorComponent
    {
        string Name { get; }

        double Value { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\IDiscoveryVectorInput.cs
################################################################
namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Represents one authoritative Discovery selected for
    /// vectorization.
    ///
    /// Provenance may carry the external source that caused this
    /// Discovery to enter the sample.
    ///
    /// Discoveries preserves that object without interpreting
    /// or owning it.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IDiscoveryVectorInput
    {
        IDiscovery Discovery { get; }

        object? Provenance { get; }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\IDiscoveryVectorPoint.cs

using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Represents an immutable point in consumer-defined
    /// Discovery geometry.
    ///
    /// A point has coordinates but no independent identity,
    /// provenance, evaluation time, or temporal motion.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IDiscoveryVectorPoint
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorComponent> Components { get; }

        int Dimension { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\IDiscoveryVectorSeries.cs
################################################################
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Provides a scoped, ordered view over immutable Discovery
    /// vector evaluations.
    ///
    /// A series does not establish a second temporal ownership
    /// model.
    ///
    /// Existing history infrastructure or a consumer may supply
    /// the ordered snapshots.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IDiscoveryVectorSeries
    {
        string Scope { get; }

        IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorInput> Inputs { get; }

        IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVector> Vectors { get; }

        IDiscoveryVector? Current { get; }

        IDiscoveryVector? Previous { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\IEvidenceContributionPolicy.cs
################################################################
namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Determines whether one validated Evidence item contributes
    /// toward proving a particular Fact.
    ///
    /// The policy may consider truth, Believability, Evidence
    /// strength, conditions, outcome, source, or any other
    /// consumer-facing information.
    ///
    /// Wilds imposes no universal relationship between
    /// Believability and truth.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IEvidenceContributionPolicy
    {
        bool Contributes(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence);
    }
}

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions

using System;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Describes one native consumer attribute observed when an Entity surface

    {
        string Name { get; }

        Type ValueType { get; }

        object? InitialValue { get; }

        bool CanRead { get; }

        bool CanWrite { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\ITruth.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Represents scoped knowledge that has congealed from the
    /// composition of Discoveries and their geometric clusters.
    ///
    /// Truth remains attributable and scoped.
    ///
    /// It is not an assertion of universal consumer reality.
    /// </summary>
    public interface ITruth :
        IIdentifier
    {
        string Name { get; }

        object? Value { get; }

        IDiscoveryVectorSeries Series { get; }

        IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryCluster> Clusters { get; }

        IReadOnlyList<IDiscovery> Discoveries { get; }

        double Stability { get; }

        double Confidence { get; }

        DateTimeOffset CongealedAt { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\ITruthCongealmentPolicy.cs
################################################################
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Consumer-supplied policy that determines whether Discovery
    /// geometry has become stable enough to produce a scoped
    /// Truth.
    /// </summary>
    public interface ITruthCongealmentPolicy
    {
        bool CanCongeal(
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryCluster> clusters,
            IDiscoveryVectorSeries series);

        ITruth Congeal(
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryCluster> clusters,
            IDiscoveryVectorSeries series);
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\IWildsProjection.cs
################################################################
using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Projects an authoritative consumer-facing domain object into Wilds as an
    /// ordinary processable object. Projection adds Wilds identity and processing
    /// participation; it does not replace or reinterpret the source.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IWildsProjection<out TSource> :
        IBaseObject
        where TSource : class
    {
        TSource Source { get; }
    }
}

    {

        }
        public IDiscoveryVectorEvaluationSeries Series { get; }
    }

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\Motion\DiscoveryVectorMotionServiceResult.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Motion
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorMotionServiceResult
    {
        public DiscoveryVectorMotionServiceResult(
            IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation evaluation,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorMotion> motion)
        {
            Evaluation = evaluation ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(evaluation));
            if (motion == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(motion));
            var copy = new IDiscoveryVectorMotion[motion.Count];
            for (var i = 0; i < motion.Count; i++)
                copy[i] = motion[i] ?? throw new ArgumentException(
                    "Motion cannot contain null entries.", nameof(motion));
            Motion = new ReadOnlyCollection<IDiscoveryVectorMotion>(copy);
        }
        public IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation Evaluation { get; }
        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorMotion> Motion { get; }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Abstractions\Vectorization\DiscoveryVectorizationServiceResult.cs

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Vectorization
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorizationServiceResult
    {
        public DiscoveryVectorizationServiceResult(
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorContribution> contributions)
        {
            if (contributions == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(contributions));
            var snapshot = new IDiscoveryVectorContribution[contributions.Count];
            for (var index = 0; index < contributions.Count; index++)
                snapshot[index] = contributions[index] ?? throw new ArgumentException(
                    "Vectorization results cannot contain null contributions.", nameof(contributions));
            Contributions = new ReadOnlyCollection<IDiscoveryVectorContribution>(snapshot);
        }
        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorContribution> Contributions { get; }
    }
}
