################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Base\ConsequenceBase.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Reusable base for projected Consequences.
    ///
    /// Consequence uses the established Discoveries identity
    /// model and remains knowledge rather than behavior.
    /// </summary>
    public abstract class ConsequenceBase :
        IdentifierBase,
        IConsequence
    {
        protected ConsequenceBase(
            string slug,
            string name,
            object? value,
            IReadOnlyList<ITruth> truths,
            double magnitude,
            double confidence,
            DateTimeOffset projectedAt)
            : base(
                slug)
        {
            Initialize(
                name,
                value,
                truths,
                magnitude,
                confidence,
                projectedAt);
        }

        protected ConsequenceBase(
            Guid id,
            string slug,
            string name,
            object? value,
            IReadOnlyList<ITruth> truths,
            double magnitude,
            double confidence,
            DateTimeOffset projectedAt)
            : base(
                id,
                slug)
        {
            Initialize(
                name,
                value,
                truths,
                magnitude,
                confidence,
                projectedAt);
        }

        public string Name { get; private set; } = null!;

        public object? Value { get; private set; }

        public IReadOnlyList<ITruth> Truths
        {
            get;
            private set;
        } = null!;

        public double Magnitude { get; private set; }

        public double Confidence { get; private set; }

        public DateTimeOffset ProjectedAt { get; private set; }

        private void Initialize(
            string name,
            object? value,
            IReadOnlyList<ITruth> truths,
            double magnitude,
            double confidence,
            DateTimeOffset projectedAt)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "Consequence name cannot be empty.",
                    nameof(name));
            }

            Name =
                name.Trim();

            Value =
                value;

            Truths =
                DiscoverySnapshots.Required(
                    truths,
                    nameof(truths));

            Magnitude =
                DiscoveryNumbers.NonNegative(
                    magnitude,
                    nameof(magnitude));

            Confidence =
                DiscoveryNumbers.Unit(
                    confidence,
                    nameof(confidence));

            ProjectedAt =
                projectedAt;
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Base\DiscoveryBase.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base
{
    public abstract class DiscoveryBase :
        IDiscovery
    {
        private readonly List<IFact> _facts =
            new List<IFact>();

        private readonly Resolution _resolution;

        protected DiscoveryBase(
            IEntity entity,
            int requiredFactCount,
            string? name = null,
            string? externalId = null)
        {
            Entity =
                entity ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(entity));

            Name =
                name;

            ExternalId =
                externalId;

            _resolution =
                new Resolution(
                    _facts,
                    requiredFactCount);
        }

        public string? ExternalId { get; }

        public string? Name { get; }

        public IEntity Entity { get; }

        public IReadOnlyList<IFact> Facts =>
            _facts;

        public IReadOnlyList<IFact> Contributors =>
            _resolution.Contributors;

        public int RequiredFactCount =>
            _resolution.RequiredCount;

        public int CurrentFactCount =>
            _resolution.CurrentCount;

        public double FactPercentage =>
            _resolution.Percentage;

        public bool IsAvailable =>
            _resolution.IsResolved;

        public int RequiredCount =>
            RequiredFactCount;

        public int CurrentCount =>
            CurrentFactCount;

        public double Percentage =>
            FactPercentage;

        public bool IsResolved =>
            IsAvailable;

        protected bool AddFact(
            IFact fact)
        {
            if (fact == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(fact));
            }

            if (!ReferenceEquals(
                    fact.Entity,
                    Entity))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "A Fact must belong to this exact Discovery Entity.",
                    nameof(fact));
            }

            if (_facts.Contains(
                    fact))
            {
                return false;
            }

            _facts.Add(
                fact);

            return true;
        }

        protected bool RemoveFact(
            IFact fact)
        {
            if (fact == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(fact));
            }

            return _facts.Remove(
                fact);
        }

        private sealed class Resolution :
            ResolutionThresholdBase<IFact>
        {
            private readonly IReadOnlyList<IFact> _items;

            public Resolution(
                IReadOnlyList<IFact> items,
                int requiredCount)
                : base(
                    requiredCount)
            {
                _items =
                    items ??
                    throw new ArgumentNullException(
                        nameof(items));
            }

            protected override IReadOnlyList<IFact> Items =>
                _items;

            protected override bool IsValidContributor(
                IFact contributor)
            {
                return contributor != null &&
                       contributor.IsProven;
            }
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Base\DiscoveryClusterBase.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Reusable immutable geometric cluster over Discovery
    /// vectors.
    ///
    /// The centroid is a geometric point, not a complete
    /// provenance-bearing vector evaluation.
    /// </summary>
    public abstract class DiscoveryClusterBase :
        IDiscoveryCluster
    {
        protected DiscoveryClusterBase(
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVector> members,
            IDiscoveryVectorPoint centroid,
            double density,
            double dispersion,
            double coherence)
        {
            if (members == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(members));
            }

            if (members.Count == 0)
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "A Discovery cluster requires at least one member.",
                    nameof(members));
            }

            Members =
                DiscoverySnapshots.Required(
                    members,
                    nameof(members));

            Centroid =
                centroid ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(centroid));

            Density =
                DiscoveryNumbers.Unit(
                    density,
                    nameof(density));

            Dispersion =
                DiscoveryNumbers.NonNegative(
                    dispersion,
                    nameof(dispersion));

            Coherence =
                DiscoveryNumbers.Unit(
                    coherence,
                    nameof(coherence));
        }

        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVector> Members { get; }

        public IDiscoveryVectorPoint Centroid { get; }

        public double Density { get; }

        public double Dispersion { get; }

        public double Coherence { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Base\DiscoveryDimensions.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Shared dimension validation for Discovery geometry.
    /// </summary>
    internal static class DiscoveryDimensions
    {
        public static void ValidateComponents(
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorComponent> components,
            string parameterName)
        {
            if (components == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    parameterName);
            }

            var names =
                new HashSet<string>(
                    StringComparer.Ordinal);

            for (var index = 0;
                 index < components.Count;
                 index++)
            {
                var component =
                    components[index] ??
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Discovery-vector components cannot contain null items.",
                        parameterName);

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                        component.Name))
                {
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Discovery-vector component names cannot be empty.",
                        parameterName);
                }

                if (!names.Add(
                        component.Name))
                {
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Discovery-vector component names must be unique.",
                        parameterName);
                }

                DiscoveryNumbers.Finite(
                    component.Value,
                    parameterName);
            }
        }

        public static void ValidateContributions(
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorContribution> contributions,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorComponent> components,
            string parameterName)
        {
            if (contributions == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    parameterName);
            }

            if (components == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(components));
            }

            var dimensions =
                new HashSet<string>(
                    StringComparer.Ordinal);

            for (var index = 0;
                 index < components.Count;
                 index++)
            {
                dimensions.Add(
                    components[index].Name);
            }

            for (var index = 0;
                 index < contributions.Count;
                 index++)
            {
                var contribution =
                    contributions[index] ??
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Discovery-vector contributions cannot contain null items.",
                        parameterName);

                if (contribution.Input == null)
                {
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Discovery-vector contributions require an input.",
                        parameterName);
                }

                if (contribution.Input.Discovery == null)
                {
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Discovery-vector inputs require a Discovery.",
                        parameterName);
                }

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                        contribution.Dimension))
                {
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Discovery-vector contribution dimensions cannot be empty.",
                        parameterName);
                }

                if (!dimensions.Contains(
                        contribution.Dimension))
                {
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Every contribution dimension must exist in the vector.",
                        parameterName);
                }

                if (contribution.Fact != null &&
                    !ReferenceEquals(
                        contribution.Fact.Entity,
                        contribution.Input.Discovery.Entity))
                {
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "A contributing Fact must belong to the same exact Entity as its Discovery.",
                        parameterName);
                }

                DiscoveryNumbers.Finite(
                    contribution.Value,
                    parameterName);

                DiscoveryNumbers.NonNegative(
                    contribution.Weight,
                    parameterName);
            }
        }

        public static void ValidateMotion(
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorMotion> motion,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorComponent> components,
            string parameterName)
        {
            if (motion == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    parameterName);
            }

            if (components == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(components));
            }

            var dimensions =
                new HashSet<string>(
                    StringComparer.Ordinal);

            for (var index = 0;
                 index < components.Count;
                 index++)
            {
                dimensions.Add(
                    components[index].Name);
            }

            var motionDimensions =
                new HashSet<string>(
                    StringComparer.Ordinal);

            for (var index = 0;
                 index < motion.Count;
                 index++)
            {
                var item =
                    motion[index] ??
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Discovery-vector motion cannot contain null items.",
                        parameterName);

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                        item.Dimension))
                {
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Discovery-vector motion dimensions cannot be empty.",
                        parameterName);
                }

                if (!dimensions.Contains(
                        item.Dimension))
                {
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Every motion dimension must exist in the vector.",
                        parameterName);
                }

                if (!motionDimensions.Add(
                        item.Dimension))
                {
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Only one motion value may exist per vector dimension.",
                        parameterName);
                }

                DiscoveryNumbers.Finite(
                    item.Velocity,
                    parameterName);

                DiscoveryNumbers.Finite(
                    item.Acceleration,
                    parameterName);
            }
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Base\DiscoveryNumbers.cs
################################################################
using System;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Shared finite-number validation for Discoveries
    /// composition results.
    /// </summary>
    internal static class DiscoveryNumbers
    {
        public static double Finite(
            double value,
            string parameterName)
        {
            if (double.IsNaN(value) ||
                double.IsInfinity(value))
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    parameterName,
                    value,
                    "Value must be finite.");
            }

            return value;
        }

        public static double NonNegative(
            double value,
            string parameterName)
        {
            Finite(
                value,
                parameterName);

            if (value < 0.0)
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    parameterName,
                    value,
                    "Value must be non-negative.");
            }

            return value;
        }

        public static double Unit(
            double value,
            string parameterName)
        {
            Finite(
                value,
                parameterName);

            if (value < 0.0 ||
                value > 1.0)
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    parameterName,
                    value,
                    "Value must be within [0,1].");
            }

            return value;
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Base\DiscoverySnapshots.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Shared immutable snapshot mechanics for Discoveries
    /// composition results.
    /// </summary>
    internal static class DiscoverySnapshots
    {
        public static IReadOnlyList<T> Required<T>(
            IReadOnlyList<T> source,
            string parameterName)
            where T : class
        {
            if (source == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    parameterName);
            }

            var snapshot =
                new T[source.Count];

            for (var index = 0;
                 index < source.Count;
                 index++)
            {
                snapshot[index] =
                    source[index] ??
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Snapshot collections cannot contain null items.",
                        parameterName);
            }

            return new ReadOnlyCollection<T>(
                snapshot);
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Base\DiscoveryVectorBase.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Reusable immutable Discovery-vector evaluation.
    ///
    /// Magnitude is calculated by the supplied metric from the
    /// exact validated component snapshot.
    /// </summary>
    public abstract class DiscoveryVectorBase :
        IDiscoveryVector
    {
        protected DiscoveryVectorBase(
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorComponent> components,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorContribution> contributions,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorMotion> motion,
            IDiscoveryVectorMetric metric,
            DateTimeOffset evaluatedAt)
        {
            Components =
                DiscoverySnapshots.Required(
                    components,
                    nameof(components));

            Contributions =
                DiscoverySnapshots.Required(
                    contributions,
                    nameof(contributions));

            Motion =
                DiscoverySnapshots.Required(
                    motion,
                    nameof(motion));

            DiscoveryDimensions.ValidateComponents(
                Components,
                nameof(components));

            DiscoveryDimensions.ValidateContributions(
                Contributions,
                Components,
                nameof(contributions));

            DiscoveryDimensions.ValidateMotion(
                Motion,
                Components,
                nameof(motion));

            Metric =
                metric ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(metric));

            EvaluatedAt =
                evaluatedAt;

            var magnitude =
                Metric.GetMagnitude(
                    this);

            Magnitude =
                DiscoveryNumbers.NonNegative(
                    magnitude,
                    nameof(magnitude));
        }

        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorComponent> Components { get; }

        public int Dimension =>
            Components.Count;

        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorContribution> Contributions
        {
            get;
        }

        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorMotion> Motion { get; }

        public IDiscoveryVectorMetric Metric { get; }

        public double Magnitude { get; }

        public DateTimeOffset EvaluatedAt { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Base\EntityBase.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base
{
    public abstract class EntityBase :
        IdentifierBase,
        IEntity
    {
        private readonly List<IFact> _facts =
            new List<IFact>();

        private readonly List<IDiscovery> _discoveries =
            new List<IDiscovery>();

        protected EntityBase(
            string slug)
            : base(
                slug)
        {
        }

        protected EntityBase(
            Guid id,
            string slug)
            : base(
                id,
                slug)
        {
        }

        public IReadOnlyList<IFact> Facts =>
            _facts;

        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscovery> Discoveries =>
            _discoveries;

        protected bool AddFact(
            IFact fact)
        {
            if (fact == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(fact));
            }

            if (!ReferenceEquals(
                    fact.Entity,
                    this))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "A Fact must belong to this exact Entity.",
                    nameof(fact));
            }

            if (_facts.Contains(
                    fact))
            {
                return false;
            }

            _facts.Add(
                fact);

            return true;
        }

        protected bool RemoveFact(
            IFact fact)
        {
            if (fact == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(fact));
            }

            return _facts.Remove(
                fact);
        }

        protected bool AddDiscovery(
            IDiscovery discovery)
        {
            if (discovery == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(discovery));
            }

            if (!ReferenceEquals(
                    discovery.Entity,
                    this))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "A Discovery must belong to this exact Entity.",
                    nameof(discovery));
            }

            for (var index = 0;
                 index < discovery.Facts.Count;
                 index++)
            {
                if (!ReferenceEquals(
                        discovery.Facts[index].Entity,
                        this))
                {
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Every Discovery Fact must belong to " +
                        "this exact Entity.",
                        nameof(discovery));
                }
            }

            if (_discoveries.Contains(
                    discovery))
            {
                return false;
            }

            _discoveries.Add(
                discovery);

            return true;
        }

        protected bool RemoveDiscovery(
            IDiscovery discovery)
        {
            if (discovery == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(discovery));
            }

            return _discoveries.Remove(
                discovery);
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Base\EntitySurfaceBase.cs
################################################################
using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base
{
    public abstract class EntitySurfaceBase :
        IEntitySurface
    {
        protected EntitySurfaceBase(
            IEntity entity,
            object target,
            string alias,
            string consumerType)
        {
            Entity =
                entity ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(entity));

            Target =
                target ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(target));

            Alias =
                RequireVocabulary(
                    alias,
                    nameof(alias));

            ConsumerType =
                RequireVocabulary(
                    consumerType,
                    nameof(consumerType));
        }

        public IEntity Entity { get; }

        public object Target { get; }

        public string Alias { get; }

        public string ConsumerType { get; }

        protected void RequireOwnedFact(
            IFact fact)
        {
            if (fact == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(fact));
            }

            if (!ReferenceEquals(
                    fact.Entity,
                    Entity))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "The Fact must belong to the exact Surface Entity.",
                    nameof(fact));
            }
        }

        protected void RequireOwnedDiscovery(
            IDiscovery discovery)
        {
            if (discovery == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(discovery));
            }

            if (!ReferenceEquals(
                    discovery.Entity,
                    Entity))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "The Discovery must belong to the exact Surface Entity.",
                    nameof(discovery));
            }

            for (var index = 0;
                 index < discovery.Facts.Count;
                 index++)
            {
                RequireOwnedFact(
                    discovery.Facts[index]);
            }
        }

        private static string RequireVocabulary(
            string value,
            string parameterName)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "Surface vocabulary cannot be empty.",
                    parameterName);
            }

            return value.Trim();
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Base\EvidenceBase.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Represents one consumer-facing observation gathered
    /// from the world.
    ///
    /// Evidence records what occurred.
    /// It does not decide which Fact it proves or which
    /// Discovery may eventually consume that Fact.
    /// </summary>
    public abstract class EvidenceBase :
        IDiscoveryEvidence
    {
        private IProofResult? _proofResult;

        protected EvidenceBase(
            IEntity entity,
            IReadOnlyDictionary<string, object?> conditions,
            object? outcome,
            double strength,
            double normalizedStrength,
            IReadOnlyList<IBaseObject> neighbors,
            IBaseUnit? source,
            string? externalId = null,
            string? name = null)
        {
            Entity =
                entity ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(entity));

            if (conditions == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(conditions));
            }

            if (neighbors == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(neighbors));
            }

            if (double.IsNaN(
                    strength) ||
                double.IsInfinity(
                    strength))
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(strength),
                    "Evidence strength must be finite.");
            }

            if (double.IsNaN(
                    normalizedStrength) ||
                double.IsInfinity(
                    normalizedStrength) ||
                normalizedStrength < 0.0 ||
                normalizedStrength > 1.0)
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(normalizedStrength),
                    "Normalized evidence strength must be " +
                    "between zero and one.");
            }

            Conditions =
                new ReadOnlyDictionary<string, object?>(
                    new Dictionary<string, object?>(
                        conditions,
                        StringComparer.Ordinal));

            Neighbors =
                new ReadOnlyCollection<IBaseObject>(
                    new List<IBaseObject>(
                        neighbors));

            Outcome =
                outcome;

            Strength =
                strength;

            NormalizedStrength =
                normalizedStrength;

            Source =
                source;

            ExternalId =
                externalId;

            Name =
                name;
        }

        public string? ExternalId { get; }

        public string? Name { get; }

        public IEntity Entity { get; }

        public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, object?>
            Conditions { get; }

        public object? Outcome { get; }

        public double Strength { get; }

        public double NormalizedStrength { get; }

        public IReadOnlyList<IBaseObject> Neighbors { get; }

        public IBaseUnit? Source { get; }

        public IProofResult ProofResult =>
            _proofResult ??
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                "Evidence has not been validated.");

        public bool IsValidated =>
            _proofResult != null;

        protected bool SetProofResult(
            IProofResult proofResult)
        {
            if (proofResult == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(proofResult));
            }

            if (!ReferenceEquals(
                    proofResult.Evidence,
                    this))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "The ProofResult must belong to this " +
                    "exact Evidence instance.",
                    nameof(proofResult));
            }

            if (_proofResult != null)
            {
                return false;
            }

            _proofResult =
                proofResult;

            return true;
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Base\FactBase.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base
{
    public abstract class FactBase :
        IFact
    {
        private readonly List<IDiscoveryEvidence> _evidence =
            new List<IDiscoveryEvidence>();

        private readonly Resolution _resolution;

        protected FactBase(
            IEntity entity,
            string name,
            object? value,
            int requiredEvidenceCount,
            IEvidenceContributionPolicy contributionPolicy,
            string? externalId = null)
        {
            Entity =
                entity ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(entity));

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    name))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "A Fact requires a name.",
                    nameof(name));
            }

            ContributionPolicy =
                contributionPolicy ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(contributionPolicy));

            Name =
                name.Trim();

            Value =
                value;

            ExternalId =
                externalId;

            _resolution =
                new Resolution(
                    _evidence,
                    requiredEvidenceCount,
                    ContributionPolicy);
        }

        public string? ExternalId { get; }

        public string Name { get; }

        public object? Value { get; private set; }

        public IEntity Entity { get; }

        public IEvidenceContributionPolicy ContributionPolicy { get; }

        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryEvidence> Evidence =>
            _evidence;

        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryEvidence> Contributors =>
            _resolution.Contributors;

        public int RequiredEvidenceCount =>
            _resolution.RequiredCount;

        public int CurrentEvidenceCount =>
            _resolution.CurrentCount;

        public double EvidencePercentage =>
            _resolution.Percentage;

        public bool IsProven =>
            _resolution.IsResolved;

        public int RequiredCount =>
            RequiredEvidenceCount;

        public int CurrentCount =>
            CurrentEvidenceCount;

        public double Percentage =>
            EvidencePercentage;

        public bool IsResolved =>
            IsProven;

        protected void SetValue(
            object? value)
        {
            Value =
                value;
        }

        protected bool AddEvidence(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)
        {
            if (evidence == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(evidence));
            }

            if (!ReferenceEquals(
                    evidence.Entity,
                    Entity))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "Evidence must belong to this exact Entity.",
                    nameof(evidence));
            }

            if (_evidence.Contains(
                    evidence))
            {
                return false;
            }

            _evidence.Add(
                evidence);

            return true;
        }

        protected bool RemoveEvidence(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)
        {
            if (evidence == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(evidence));
            }

            return _evidence.Remove(
                evidence);
        }

        private sealed class Resolution :
            ResolutionThresholdBase<IDiscoveryEvidence>
        {
            private readonly IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryEvidence> _items;

            private readonly IEvidenceContributionPolicy _policy;

            public Resolution(
                IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryEvidence> items,
                int requiredCount,
                IEvidenceContributionPolicy policy)
                : base(
                    requiredCount)
            {
                _items =
                    items ??
                    throw new ArgumentNullException(
                        nameof(items));

                _policy =
                    policy ??
                    throw new ArgumentNullException(
                        nameof(policy));
            }

            protected override IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryEvidence> Items =>
                _items;

            protected override bool IsValidContributor(
                IDiscoveryEvidence contributor)
            {
                if (contributor == null ||
                    !contributor.IsValidated)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                if (!ReferenceEquals(
                        contributor.ProofResult.Evidence,
                        contributor))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                return _policy.Contributes(
                    contributor);
            }
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Base\ProofBase.cs
################################################################
using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Proofs;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Provides the reusable Evidence validation lifecycle.
    ///
    /// Concrete Proof implementations determine:
    ///
    /// - how believable the Evidence is,
    /// - whether the Evidence resolves as true,
    /// - and any consumer-facing reason for that conclusion.
    ///
    /// Proof does not decide which Fact or Discovery
    /// will consume the Evidence.
    /// </summary>
    public abstract class ProofBase :
        IProof
    {
        public IProofResult Validate(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)
        {
            if (evidence == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(evidence));
            }

            ResolveEvidence(
                evidence,
                out var believability,
                out var isTrue,
                out var reason);

            if (double.IsNaN(
                    believability) ||
                double.IsInfinity(
                    believability) ||
                believability < 0.0 ||
                believability > 1.0)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Proof resolution produced an invalid " +
                    "Believability value.");
            }

            return new ProofResult(
                evidence,
                this,
                believability,
                isTrue,
                reason);
        }

        protected abstract void ResolveEvidence(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence,
            out double believability,
            out bool isTrue,
            out string? reason);
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Base\ResolutionThresholdBase.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base
{
    public abstract class ResolutionThresholdBase<TContributor>
    {
        protected ResolutionThresholdBase(
            int requiredCount)
        {
            if (requiredCount <= 0)
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(requiredCount),
                    "Resolution requires at least one contributor.");
            }

            RequiredCount =
                requiredCount;
        }

        protected abstract IReadOnlyList<TContributor> Items { get; }

        public IReadOnlyList<TContributor> Contributors =>
            Items;

        public int RequiredCount { get; }

        public int CurrentCount
        {
            get
            {
                var count = 0;

                for (var index = 0;
                     index < Items.Count;
                     index++)
                {
                    if (IsValidContributor(Items[index]))
                    {
                        count++;
                    }
                }

                return count;
            }
        }

        public double Percentage =>
            Math.Min(
                1.0,
                (double)CurrentCount / RequiredCount);

        public bool IsResolved =>
            CurrentCount >= RequiredCount;

        protected abstract bool IsValidContributor(
            TContributor contributor);
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Base\TruthBase.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Reusable base for scoped Truths congealed from Discovery
    /// composition.
    ///
    /// Truth reuses established Discoveries identity and retains
    /// the exact scoped vector series whose composition
    /// congealed.
    /// </summary>
    public abstract class TruthBase :
        IdentifierBase,
        ITruth
    {
        protected TruthBase(
            string slug,
            string name,
            object? value,
            IDiscoveryVectorSeries series,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryCluster> clusters,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscovery> discoveries,
            double stability,
            double confidence,
            DateTimeOffset congealedAt)
            : base(
                slug)
        {
            Initialize(
                name,
                value,
                series,
                clusters,
                discoveries,
                stability,
                confidence,
                congealedAt);
        }

        protected TruthBase(
            Guid id,
            string slug,
            string name,
            object? value,
            IDiscoveryVectorSeries series,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryCluster> clusters,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscovery> discoveries,
            double stability,
            double confidence,
            DateTimeOffset congealedAt)
            : base(
                id,
                slug)
        {
            Initialize(
                name,
                value,
                series,
                clusters,
                discoveries,
                stability,
                confidence,
                congealedAt);
        }

        public string Name { get; private set; } = null!;

        public object? Value { get; private set; }

        public IDiscoveryVectorSeries Series
        {
            get;
            private set;
        } = null!;

        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryCluster> Clusters
        {
            get;
            private set;
        } = null!;

        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscovery> Discoveries
        {
            get;
            private set;
        } = null!;

        public double Stability { get; private set; }

        public double Confidence { get; private set; }

        public DateTimeOffset CongealedAt { get; private set; }

        private void Initialize(
            string name,
            object? value,
            IDiscoveryVectorSeries series,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryCluster> clusters,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscovery> discoveries,
            double stability,
            double confidence,
            DateTimeOffset congealedAt)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "Truth name cannot be empty.",
                    nameof(name));
            }

            Name =
                name.Trim();

            Value =
                value;

            Series =
                series ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(series));

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    Series.Scope))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "Truth requires a scoped Discovery-vector series.",
                    nameof(series));
            }

            if (Series.Inputs == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "Truth requires a series with an input population.",
                    nameof(series));
            }

            if (Series.Vectors == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "Truth requires a series with vector evaluations.",
                    nameof(series));
            }

            Clusters =
                DiscoverySnapshots.Required(
                    clusters,
                    nameof(clusters));

            Discoveries =
                DiscoverySnapshots.Required(
                    discoveries,
                    nameof(discoveries));

            ValidateDiscoveriesBelongToSeries(
                Discoveries,
                Series);

            Stability =
                DiscoveryNumbers.Unit(
                    stability,
                    nameof(stability));

            Confidence =
                DiscoveryNumbers.Unit(
                    confidence,
                    nameof(confidence));

            CongealedAt =
                congealedAt;
        }

        private static void ValidateDiscoveriesBelongToSeries(
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscovery> discoveries,
            IDiscoveryVectorSeries series)
        {
            for (var discoveryIndex = 0;
                 discoveryIndex < discoveries.Count;
                 discoveryIndex++)
            {
                var discovery =
                    discoveries[discoveryIndex];

                var found =
                    false;

                for (var inputIndex = 0;
                     inputIndex < series.Inputs.Count;
                     inputIndex++)
                {
                    var input =
                        series.Inputs[inputIndex];

                    if (input == null)
                    {
                        throw new ArgumentException(
                            "Truth series inputs cannot contain null items.",
                            nameof(series));
                    }

                    if (ReferenceEquals(
                            discovery,
                            input.Discovery))
                    {
                        found =
                            true;
                        break;
                    }
                }

                if (!found)
                {
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Every Truth Discovery must belong to the exact congealment series.",
                        nameof(discoveries));
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Clusters\DiscoveryClusterMapping.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Clusters;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Clusters
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryClusterMapping : IdentifierBase, IDiscoveryClusterMapping
    {
        public DiscoveryClusterMapping(
            Guid id, string slug, Guid documentId, string documentSlug,
            string documentVersion, string semanticKey, string geometryType,
            IReadOnlyDictionary<string, double> centroid, double radius,
            IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string>? metadata = null)
            : base(id, slug)
        {
            if (documentId == Guid.Empty) throw new ArgumentException("Document ID is required.", nameof(documentId));
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(documentSlug)) throw new ArgumentException("Document slug is required.", nameof(documentSlug));
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(documentVersion)) throw new ArgumentException("Document version is required.", nameof(documentVersion));
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(semanticKey)) throw new ArgumentException("Semantic key is required.", nameof(semanticKey));
            if (!string.Equals(geometryType, "hypersphere", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                throw new ArgumentException("Only hypersphere geometry is supported by H3A.", nameof(geometryType));
            if (centroid == null || centroid.Count == 0) throw new ArgumentException("Centroid dimensions are required.", nameof(centroid));
            if (double.IsNaN(radius) || double.IsInfinity(radius) || radius <= 0.0)
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(radius));
            var centroidCopy = new Dictionary<string, double>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            foreach (var pair in centroid)
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(pair.Key)) throw new ArgumentException("Centroid dimension names cannot be empty.", nameof(centroid));
                if (double.IsNaN(pair.Value) || double.IsInfinity(pair.Value)) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(centroid));
                centroidCopy.Add(pair.Key.Trim(), pair.Value);
            }
            var metadataCopy = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            if (metadata != null) foreach (var pair in metadata) metadataCopy.Add(pair.Key, pair.Value);
            DocumentId = documentId;
            DocumentSlug = documentSlug.Trim();
            DocumentVersion = documentVersion.Trim();
            SemanticKey = semanticKey.Trim();
            GeometryType = geometryType;
            Centroid = new ReadOnlyDictionary<string, double>(centroidCopy);
            Radius = radius;
            Metadata = new ReadOnlyDictionary<string, string>(metadataCopy);
        }
        public Guid DocumentId { get; }
        public string DocumentSlug { get; }
        public string DocumentVersion { get; }
        public string SemanticKey { get; }
        public string GeometryType { get; }
        public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, double> Centroid { get; }
        public double Radius { get; }
        public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> Metadata { get; }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Clusters\DiscoveryClusterMappingDocument.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Clusters;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Clusters
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryClusterMappingDocument : IdentifierBase, IDiscoveryClusterMappingDocument
    {
        public DiscoveryClusterMappingDocument(
            Guid id, string slug, string version, string sourceName,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryClusterMapping> mappings)
            : base(id, slug)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(version)) throw new ArgumentException("Version is required.", nameof(version));
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sourceName)) throw new ArgumentException("Source name is required.", nameof(sourceName));
            if (mappings == null || mappings.Count == 0) throw new ArgumentException("At least one mapping is required.", nameof(mappings));
            var copy = new IDiscoveryClusterMapping[mappings.Count];
            for (var i = 0; i < mappings.Count; i++) copy[i] = mappings[i] ?? throw new ArgumentException("Mappings cannot contain null.", nameof(mappings));
            Version = version.Trim();
            SourceName = sourceName.Trim();
            Mappings = new ReadOnlyCollection<IDiscoveryClusterMapping>(copy);
        }
        public string Version { get; }
        public string SourceName { get; }
        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryClusterMapping> Mappings { get; }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Clusters\DiscoveryClusterMappingImportService.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Clusters;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Services;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Clusters
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryClusterMappingImportService :
        WildsServiceBase<DiscoveryClusterMappingImportRequest, DiscoveryClusterMappingImportResult>,
        IDiscoveryClusterMappingImportService
    {
        private readonly IDiscoveryClusterMappingRegistry _registry;
        public DiscoveryClusterMappingImportService(
            string slug,
            IDiscoveryClusterMappingRegistry registry,
            IWildsServiceContextFactory<IWildsServiceContext> contexts,
            IWildsServiceExecutionJournal journal,
            IWildsServiceClock clock)
            : base(slug, contexts, journal, clock)
        {
            _registry = registry ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(registry));
        }
        protected override Task<DiscoveryClusterMappingImportResult> ExecuteCoreAsync(
            DiscoveryClusterMappingImportRequest request,
            IWildsServiceContext context,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
            var document = DiscoveryClusterMappingJsonParser.Parse(request.SourceName, request.DocumentContent);
            cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
            _registry.RegisterDocument(document);
            return Task.FromResult(new DiscoveryClusterMappingImportResult(
                document.Id, document.Slug, document.Mappings.Count));
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Clusters\DiscoveryClusterMappingJsonParser.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Runtime.Serialization;
using System.Runtime.Serialization.Json;
using System.Text;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Clusters;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Clusters
{
    internal static class DiscoveryClusterMappingJsonParser
    {
        public static IDiscoveryClusterMappingDocument Parse(string sourceName, string content)
        {
            try
            {
                var serializer = new DataContractJsonSerializer(
                    typeof(DocumentDto),
                    new DataContractJsonSerializerSettings
                    {
                        UseSimpleDictionaryFormat = true
                    });
                using var stream = new MemoryStream(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(content));
                var dto = serializer.ReadObject(stream) as DocumentDto ?? throw new SerializationException("Document is empty.");
                var documentId = ParseGuid(dto.Id, "document id");
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dto.Slug)) throw new SerializationException("Document slug is required.");
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dto.Version)) throw new SerializationException("Document version is required.");
                if (dto.Mappings == null || dto.Mappings.Count == 0) throw new SerializationException("At least one mapping is required.");
                var mappings = new List<IDiscoveryClusterMapping>(dto.Mappings.Count);
                foreach (var item in dto.Mappings)
                {
                    if (item == null) throw new SerializationException("Mapping cannot be null.");
                    mappings.Add(new DiscoveryClusterMapping(
                        ParseGuid(item.Id, "cluster id"), item.Slug ?? string.Empty,
                        documentId, dto.Slug, dto.Version,
                        item.SemanticKey ?? string.Empty,
                        item.GeometryType ?? string.Empty,
                        item.Centroid ?? new Dictionary<string, double>(),
                        item.Radius,
                        item.Metadata));
                }
                return new DiscoveryClusterMappingDocument(documentId, dto.Slug, dto.Version, sourceName, mappings);
            }
            catch (Exception exception) when (!(exception is ArgumentException))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException("Cluster mapping document is invalid.", nameof(content), exception);
            }
        }
        private static Guid ParseGuid(string? value, string field)
        {
            if (!Guid.TryParse(value, out var parsed) || parsed == Guid.Empty)
                throw new SerializationException("Invalid " + field + ".");
            return parsed;
        }

        [DataContract]
        private sealed class DocumentDto
        {
            [DataMember(Name="id")] public string? Id { get; set; }
            [DataMember(Name="slug")] public string? Slug { get; set; }
            [DataMember(Name="version")] public string? Version { get; set; }
            [DataMember(Name="mappings")] public List<MappingDto>? Mappings { get; set; }
        }
        [DataContract]
        private sealed class MappingDto
        {
            [DataMember(Name="id")] public string? Id { get; set; }
            [DataMember(Name="slug")] public string? Slug { get; set; }
            [DataMember(Name="semanticKey")] public string? SemanticKey { get; set; }
            [DataMember(Name="geometryType")] public string? GeometryType { get; set; }
            [DataMember(Name="centroid")] public Dictionary<string,double>? Centroid { get; set; }
            [DataMember(Name="radius")] public double Radius { get; set; }
            [DataMember(Name="metadata")] public Dictionary<string,string>? Metadata { get; set; }
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Clusters\DiscoveryClusterSpatialRelationship.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Clusters;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Motion;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Clusters
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryClusterSpatialRelationship : IDiscoveryClusterSpatialRelationship
    {
        public DiscoveryClusterSpatialRelationship(
            IDiscoveryClusterMapping mapping,
            IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation evaluation,
            double distanceToCentroid,
            double signedDistanceToBoundary,
            bool isInside,
            bool isOnBoundary,
            IReadOnlyDictionary<string, double> nearestBoundaryPoint)
        {
            Mapping = mapping ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(mapping));
            Evaluation = evaluation ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(evaluation));
            DistanceToCentroid = Finite(distanceToCentroid, nameof(distanceToCentroid));
            SignedDistanceToBoundary = Finite(signedDistanceToBoundary, nameof(signedDistanceToBoundary));
            IsInside = isInside;
            IsOnBoundary = isOnBoundary;
            if (nearestBoundaryPoint == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(nearestBoundaryPoint));
            NearestBoundaryPoint = new ReadOnlyDictionary<string, double>(
                new Dictionary<string, double>(nearestBoundaryPoint, StringComparer.Ordinal));
        }
        public IDiscoveryClusterMapping Mapping { get; }
        public IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation Evaluation { get; }
        public double DistanceToCentroid { get; }
        public double SignedDistanceToBoundary { get; }
        public bool IsInside { get; }
        public bool IsOnBoundary { get; }
        public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, double> NearestBoundaryPoint { get; }
        private static double Finite(double value, string name)
        { if (double.IsNaN(value) || double.IsInfinity(value)) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(name); return value; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Clusters\DiscoveryClusterTrajectoryProjection.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Clusters;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Motion;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Clusters
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryClusterTrajectoryProjection : IDiscoveryClusterTrajectoryProjection
    {
        public DiscoveryClusterTrajectoryProjection(
            IDiscoveryClusterSpatialRelationship spatial,
            IDiscoveryVectorMotionResult motion,
            DiscoveryClusterTransitKind transitKind,
            double radialRate,
            double timeToClosestApproachSeconds,
            double closestApproachDistanceToCentroid,
            double closestApproachDistanceToBoundary,
            double? timeToEntrySeconds,
            double? timeToExitSeconds,
            double? projectedDurationInsideSeconds,
            IReadOnlyDictionary<string,double>? entryPoint,
            IReadOnlyDictionary<string,double>? exitPoint)
        {
            Spatial = spatial ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(spatial));
            Motion = motion ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(motion));
            ProjectionBasis = "constant-velocity";
            TransitKind = transitKind;
            RadialRate = radialRate;
            IsApproaching = radialRate < 0.0;
            IsReceding = radialRate > 0.0;
            TimeToClosestApproachSeconds = timeToClosestApproachSeconds;
            ClosestApproachDistanceToCentroid = closestApproachDistanceToCentroid;
            ClosestApproachDistanceToBoundary = closestApproachDistanceToBoundary;
            TimeToEntrySeconds = timeToEntrySeconds;
            TimeToExitSeconds = timeToExitSeconds;
            ProjectedDurationInsideSeconds = projectedDurationInsideSeconds;
            EntryPoint = Copy(entryPoint);
            ExitPoint = Copy(exitPoint);
        }
        public IDiscoveryClusterSpatialRelationship Spatial { get; }
        public IDiscoveryVectorMotionResult Motion { get; }
        public string ProjectionBasis { get; }
        public DiscoveryClusterTransitKind TransitKind { get; }
        public double RadialRate { get; }
        public bool IsApproaching { get; }
        public bool IsReceding { get; }
        public double TimeToClosestApproachSeconds { get; }
        public double ClosestApproachDistanceToCentroid { get; }
        public double ClosestApproachDistanceToBoundary { get; }
        public double? TimeToEntrySeconds { get; }
        public double? TimeToExitSeconds { get; }
        public double? ProjectedDurationInsideSeconds { get; }
        public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, double>? EntryPoint { get; }
        public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, double>? ExitPoint { get; }
        private static IReadOnlyDictionary<string,double>? Copy(IReadOnlyDictionary<string,double>? source)
        { return source == null ? null : new ReadOnlyDictionary<string,double>(new Dictionary<string,double>(source,StringComparer.Ordinal)); }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Clusters\HypersphereDiscoveryClusterRelationshipProjector.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Clusters;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Motion;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Clusters
{
    public sealed class HypersphereDiscoveryClusterRelationshipProjector : IDiscoveryClusterRelationshipProjector
    {
        private readonly double _epsilon;
        public HypersphereDiscoveryClusterRelationshipProjector(double epsilon = 1e-10)
        {
            if (double.IsNaN(epsilon) || double.IsInfinity(epsilon) || epsilon < 0.0)
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(epsilon));
            _epsilon = epsilon;
        }

        public IDiscoveryClusterSpatialRelationship Locate(
            IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation evaluation,
            IDiscoveryClusterMapping mapping)
        {
            Validate(evaluation, mapping);
            var position = Position(evaluation.Vector, mapping);
            var delta = Delta(position, mapping.Centroid);
            var distance = Magnitude(delta);
            var signed = distance - mapping.Radius;
            var onBoundary = Math.Abs(signed) <= _epsilon;
            var nearest = new Dictionary<string,double>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            foreach (var pair in mapping.Centroid)
            {
                var direction = distance <= _epsilon ? FirstAxis(pair.Key, mapping.Centroid) : delta[pair.Key] / distance;
                nearest[pair.Key] = pair.Value + (direction * mapping.Radius);
            }
            return new DiscoveryClusterSpatialRelationship(
                mapping, evaluation, distance, signed, signed < -_epsilon, onBoundary, nearest);
        }

        public IDiscoveryClusterTrajectoryProjection Project(
            IDiscoveryVectorMotionResult motion,
            IDiscoveryClusterMapping mapping)
        {
            if (motion == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(motion));
            var spatial = Locate(motion.Evaluation, mapping);
            var position = Position(motion.Evaluation.Vector, mapping);
            var velocity = Velocity(motion.Motion, mapping);
            var offset = Delta(position, mapping.Centroid);
            var speedSquared = Dot(velocity, velocity);
            var radialRate = spatial.DistanceToCentroid <= _epsilon ? 0.0 : Dot(offset, velocity) / spatial.DistanceToCentroid;
            if (speedSquared <= _epsilon)
                return BuildStationary(spatial, motion, radialRate);

            var closestTime = Math.Max(0.0, -Dot(offset, velocity) / speedSquared);
            var closestPosition = At(position, velocity, closestTime);
            var closestDistance = Magnitude(Delta(closestPosition, mapping.Centroid));

            var b = 2.0 * Dot(offset, velocity);
            var c = Dot(offset, offset) - (mapping.Radius * mapping.Radius);
            var discriminant = (b * b) - (4.0 * speedSquared * c);
            double? entry = null;
            double? exit = null;
            IReadOnlyDictionary<string,double>? entryPoint = null;
            IReadOnlyDictionary<string,double>? exitPoint = null;

            if (discriminant >= -_epsilon)
            {
                discriminant = Math.Max(0.0, discriminant);
                var root = Math.Sqrt(discriminant);
                var t1 = (-b - root) / (2.0 * speedSquared);
                var t2 = (-b + root) / (2.0 * speedSquared);
                if (spatial.IsInside || spatial.IsOnBoundary)
                {
                    entry = 0.0;
                    if (t2 >= 0.0) exit = t2;
                }
                else
                {
                    if (t1 >= 0.0) entry = t1;
                    if (t2 >= 0.0) exit = t2;
                }
                if (entry.HasValue) entryPoint = At(position, velocity, entry.Value);
                if (exit.HasValue) exitPoint = At(position, velocity, exit.Value);
            }

            double? duration =
                entry.HasValue && exit.HasValue
                    ? (double?)Math.Max(0.0, exit.Value - entry.Value)
                    : null;
            var kind = Classify(spatial, radialRate, entry, exit, closestDistance, mapping.Radius);
            return new DiscoveryClusterTrajectoryProjection(
                spatial, motion, kind, radialRate, closestTime, closestDistance,
                closestDistance - mapping.Radius, entry, exit, duration, entryPoint, exitPoint);
        }

        private IDiscoveryClusterTrajectoryProjection BuildStationary(
            IDiscoveryClusterSpatialRelationship spatial,
            IDiscoveryVectorMotionResult motion,
            double radialRate)
        {
            return new DiscoveryClusterTrajectoryProjection(
                spatial,
                motion,
                spatial.IsInside
                    ? DiscoveryClusterTransitKind.RemainingInside
                    : DiscoveryClusterTransitKind.Stationary,
                radialRate,
                0.0,
                spatial.DistanceToCentroid,
                spatial.SignedDistanceToBoundary,
                spatial.IsInside ? (double?)0.0 : null,
                null,
                null,
                null,
                null);
        }

        private DiscoveryClusterTransitKind Classify(
            IDiscoveryClusterSpatialRelationship spatial, double radialRate,
            double? entry, double? exit, double closestDistance, double radius)
        {
            if (spatial.IsInside || spatial.IsOnBoundary)
                return exit.HasValue ? DiscoveryClusterTransitKind.Exiting : DiscoveryClusterTransitKind.RemainingInside;
            if (entry.HasValue && exit.HasValue) return DiscoveryClusterTransitKind.PassingThrough;
            if (Math.Abs(closestDistance - radius) <= _epsilon) return DiscoveryClusterTransitKind.Flyby;
            if (radialRate < -_epsilon) return DiscoveryClusterTransitKind.Approaching;
            if (radialRate > _epsilon) return DiscoveryClusterTransitKind.Receding;
            return DiscoveryClusterTransitKind.Stationary;
        }

        private static void Validate(IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation evaluation, IDiscoveryClusterMapping mapping)
        {
            if (evaluation == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(evaluation));
            if (mapping == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(mapping));
            if (!string.Equals(mapping.GeometryType, "hypersphere", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                throw new NotSupportedException("This projector supports hypersphere mappings only.");
        }
        private static Dictionary<string,double> Position(IDiscoveryVector vector, IDiscoveryClusterMapping mapping)
        {
            var source = new Dictionary<string,double>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            for (var i=0;i<vector.Components.Count;i++) source[vector.Components[i].Name]=vector.Components[i].Value;
            var result = new Dictionary<string,double>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            foreach(var dimension in mapping.Centroid.Keys)
            { if(!source.TryGetValue(dimension,out var value)) throw new InvalidOperationException("Vector is missing mapped dimension: "+dimension); result[dimension]=value; }
            return result;
        }
        private static Dictionary<string,double> Velocity(IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorMotion> motion, IDiscoveryClusterMapping mapping)
        {
            var source=new Dictionary<string,double>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            for(var i=0;i<motion.Count;i++) source[motion[i].Dimension]=motion[i].Velocity;
            var result=new Dictionary<string,double>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            foreach(var dimension in mapping.Centroid.Keys)
            { if(!source.TryGetValue(dimension,out var value)) throw new InvalidOperationException("Motion is missing mapped dimension: "+dimension); result[dimension]=value; }
            return result;
        }
        private static Dictionary<string,double> Delta(IReadOnlyDictionary<string,double> a,IReadOnlyDictionary<string,double> b)
        { var r=new Dictionary<string,double>(StringComparer.Ordinal);foreach(var p in b) r[p.Key]=a[p.Key]-p.Value;return r; }
        private static double Dot(IReadOnlyDictionary<string,double> a,IReadOnlyDictionary<string,double> b)
        { var t=0.0;foreach(var p in a)t+=p.Value*b[p.Key];return t; }
        private static double Magnitude(IReadOnlyDictionary<string,double> v)=>Math.Sqrt(Dot(v,v));
        private static Dictionary<string,double> At(IReadOnlyDictionary<string,double> p,IReadOnlyDictionary<string,double> v,double t)
        { var r=new Dictionary<string,double>(StringComparer.Ordinal);foreach(var x in p)r[x.Key]=x.Value+(v[x.Key]*t);return r; }
        private static double FirstAxis(string key,IReadOnlyDictionary<string,double> centroid)
        { foreach(var p in centroid)return string.Equals(p.Key,key,StringComparison.Ordinal)?1.0:0.0;return 0.0; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Curves\BoundedTanhDiscoveryMotionCurve.cs
################################################################
using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Curves;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Curves
{
    public sealed class BoundedTanhDiscoveryMotionCurve : IDiscoveryMotionCurve
    {
        private readonly double _gain;
        private readonly double _limit;
        public BoundedTanhDiscoveryMotionCurve(double gain, double limit)
        {
            if (double.IsNaN(gain) || double.IsInfinity(gain) || gain <= 0.0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(gain));
            if (double.IsNaN(limit) || double.IsInfinity(limit) || limit <= 0.0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(limit));
            _gain = gain; _limit = limit;
        }
        public string Slug => "bounded-tanh";
        public double TransformVelocity(string dimension, double velocity) => Transform(velocity);
        public double TransformAcceleration(string dimension, double acceleration) => Transform(acceleration);
        private double Transform(double value)
        {
            var result = _limit * Math.Tanh(_gain * value);
            if (double.IsNaN(result) || double.IsInfinity(result)) throw new InvalidOperationException("Curve output must be finite.");
            return result;
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Curves\DiscoveryVectorMomentumProjector.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Curves;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Motion;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectors;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Curves
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorMomentumProjector : IDiscoveryVectorMomentumProjector
    {
        public IDiscoveryVectorMomentumResult Project(
            IDiscoveryVectorMotionResult motion, IDiscoveryMotionCurve curve)
        {
            if (motion == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(motion));
            if (curve == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(curve));
            var adjusted = new List<IDiscoveryVectorMotion>(motion.Motion.Count);
            for (var i = 0; i < motion.Motion.Count; i++)
            {
                var source = motion.Motion[i];
                var velocity = curve.TransformVelocity(source.Dimension, source.Velocity);
                var acceleration = curve.TransformAcceleration(source.Dimension, source.Acceleration);
                if (double.IsNaN(velocity) || double.IsInfinity(velocity) || double.IsNaN(acceleration) || double.IsInfinity(acceleration))
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("Curve output must be finite.");
                adjusted.Add(new DiscoveryVectorMotion(source.Dimension, velocity, acceleration));
            }
            return new DiscoveryVectorMomentumResult(motion, adjusted, curve.Slug);
        }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Curves\DiscoveryVectorMomentumResult.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Curves;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Motion;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Curves
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorMomentumResult : IDiscoveryVectorMomentumResult
    {
        public DiscoveryVectorMomentumResult(
            IDiscoveryVectorMotionResult motion,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorMotion> adjusted,
            string curveSlug)
        {
            if (motion == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(motion));
            if (adjusted == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(adjusted));
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(curveSlug)) throw new ArgumentException("Curve slug required.", nameof(curveSlug));
            var copy = new IDiscoveryVectorMotion[adjusted.Count];
            for (var i = 0; i < adjusted.Count; i++) copy[i] = adjusted[i];
            MotionServiceExecutionId = motion.ServiceExecutionId; Evaluation = motion.Evaluation;
            RawMotion = motion.Motion; AdjustedMotion = new ReadOnlyCollection<IDiscoveryVectorMotion>(copy); CurveSlug = curveSlug;
        }
        public Guid MotionServiceExecutionId { get; }
        public IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation Evaluation { get; }
        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorMotion> RawMotion { get; }
        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorMotion> AdjustedMotion { get; }
        public string CurveSlug { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Evidences\DiscoveryEvidence.cs
