using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Core.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Evidences
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Concrete consumer-facing Evidence gathered from
    /// one resolved world observation.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class DiscoveryEvidence :
        EvidenceBase
    {
        public DiscoveryEvidence(
            IEntity entity,
            IReadOnlyDictionary<string, object?> conditions,
            object? outcome,
            double strength,
            double normalizedStrength,
            IReadOnlyList<IBaseObject> neighbors,
            IBaseUnit? source,
            string? externalId = null,
            string? name = null)
            : base(
                entity,
                conditions,
                outcome,
                strength,
                normalizedStrength,
                neighbors,
                source,
                externalId,
                name)
        {
        }

        public bool ApplyProof(
            IProofResult proofResult)
        {
            return SetProofResult(
                proofResult);
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Motion\DiscoveryVectorEvaluationSeries.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Motion;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Motion
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorEvaluationSeries : IDiscoveryVectorEvaluationSeries
    {
        public DiscoveryVectorEvaluationSeries(
            string scope,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorInput> inputs,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation> evaluations)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(scope)) throw new ArgumentException("Series requires a scope.", nameof(scope));
            if (inputs == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(inputs));
            if (evaluations == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(evaluations));
            if (inputs.Count == 0) throw new ArgumentException("Series requires at least one input.", nameof(inputs));
            if (evaluations.Count == 0) throw new ArgumentException("Series requires at least one evaluation.", nameof(evaluations));
            var inputCopy = new IDiscoveryVectorInput[inputs.Count];
            for (var i = 0; i < inputs.Count; i++) inputCopy[i] = inputs[i] ?? throw new ArgumentException("Inputs cannot contain null.", nameof(inputs));
            var evalCopy = new IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation[evaluations.Count];
            DateTimeOffset? prior = null;
            for (var i = 0; i < evaluations.Count; i++)
            {
                var evaluation = evaluations[i] ?? throw new ArgumentException("Evaluations cannot contain null.", nameof(evaluations));
                if (prior.HasValue && evaluation.EvaluatedAt <= prior.Value)
                    throw new ArgumentException("Evaluation timestamps must be strictly increasing.", nameof(evaluations));
                ValidateInputs(inputCopy, evaluation);
                evalCopy[i] = evaluation; prior = evaluation.EvaluatedAt;
            }
            Scope = scope.Trim();
            Inputs = new ReadOnlyCollection<IDiscoveryVectorInput>(inputCopy);
            Evaluations = new ReadOnlyCollection<IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation>(evalCopy);
        }
        public string Scope { get; }
        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorInput> Inputs { get; }
        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation> Evaluations { get; }
        public IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation? Current => Evaluations.Count == 0 ? null : Evaluations[Evaluations.Count - 1];
        public IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation? Previous => Evaluations.Count < 2 ? null : Evaluations[Evaluations.Count - 2];

        private static void ValidateInputs(
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorInput> inputs,
            IDiscoveryVectorEvaluation evaluation)
        {
            for (var c = 0; c < evaluation.Vector.Contributions.Count; c++)
            {
                var candidate = evaluation.Vector.Contributions[c].Input;
                var found = false;
                for (var i = 0; i < inputs.Count; i++)
                    if (ReferenceEquals(candidate, inputs[i])) { found = true; break; }
                if (!found) throw new ArgumentException("Evaluation contains a contribution from outside the series input population.");
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Motion\DiscoveryVectorMotionEvaluator.cs

using System;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Motion;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Motion
{
    internal sealed class DiscoveryVectorMotionEvaluator : IDiscoveryVectorMotionEvaluator
    {
        private readonly IDiscoveryVectorMotionService _service;
        public DiscoveryVectorMotionEvaluator(IDiscoveryVectorMotionService service)
        { _service = service ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(service)); }
        public async Task<IDiscoveryVectorMotionResult> EvaluateAsync(
            IDiscoveryVectorEvaluationSeries series, object? provenance = null,
            Guid? parentExecutionId = null, Guid? pulseId = null,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            if (series == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(series));
            var result = await _service.ExecuteAsync(
                new DiscoveryVectorMotionRequest(series), provenance,
                parentExecutionId, pulseId, cancellationToken);
            return new DiscoveryVectorMotionResult(result.ExecutionId, result.Value);
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Motion\FiniteDifferenceDiscoveryVectorMotionService.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Motion;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Services;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectors;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Motion
{
    public sealed class FiniteDifferenceDiscoveryVectorMotionService : DiscoveryVectorMotionServiceBase
    {
        public FiniteDifferenceDiscoveryVectorMotionService(
            string slug,
            IWildsServiceContextFactory<IWildsServiceContext> contexts,
            IWildsServiceExecutionJournal journal,
            IWildsServiceClock clock)
            : base(slug, contexts, journal, clock) { }

        protected override Task<DiscoveryVectorMotionServiceResult> ExecuteCoreAsync(
            DiscoveryVectorMotionRequest request,
            IWildsServiceContext context,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
            var series = request.Series;
            var count = series.Evaluations.Count;
            var current = series.Evaluations[count - 1];
            if (count < 2)
                return Task.FromResult(new DiscoveryVectorMotionServiceResult(
                    current, new List<IDiscoveryVectorMotion>()));

            var previous = series.Evaluations[count - 2];
            var dt = Seconds(previous.EvaluatedAt, current.EvaluatedAt);
            var prior = count >= 3 ? series.Evaluations[count - 3] : null;
            var priorDt = prior == null ? 0.0 : Seconds(prior.EvaluatedAt, previous.EvaluatedAt);
            var currentMap = Map(current.Vector);
            var previousMap = Map(previous.Vector);
            var priorMap = prior == null ? null : Map(prior.Vector);
            var motion = new List<IDiscoveryVectorMotion>();

            foreach (var pair in currentMap)
            {
                if (!previousMap.TryGetValue(pair.Key, out var previousValue)) continue;
                var velocity = Finite((pair.Value - previousValue) / dt, "velocity");
                var acceleration = 0.0;
                if (priorMap != null && priorMap.TryGetValue(pair.Key, out var priorValue))
                {
                    var previousVelocity = Finite((previousValue - priorValue) / priorDt, "previous velocity");
                    acceleration = Finite((velocity - previousVelocity) / dt, "acceleration");
                }
                motion.Add(new DiscoveryVectorMotion(pair.Key, velocity, acceleration));
            }
            return Task.FromResult(new DiscoveryVectorMotionServiceResult(current, motion));
        }

        private static double Seconds(DateTimeOffset earlier, DateTimeOffset later)
        {
            var seconds = (later - earlier).TotalSeconds;
            if (double.IsNaN(seconds) || double.IsInfinity(seconds) || seconds <= 0.0)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Motion requires strictly increasing evaluation times.");
            return seconds;
        }
        private static SortedDictionary<string,double> Map(IDiscoveryVector vector)
        {
            var map = new SortedDictionary<string,double>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            for (var i = 0; i < vector.Components.Count; i++)
                map.Add(vector.Components[i].Name, vector.Components[i].Value);
            return map;
        }
        private static double Finite(double value, string name)
        {
            if (double.IsNaN(value) || double.IsInfinity(value))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Derived " + name + " must be finite.");
            return value;
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Policies\AllEvidencePolicies.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Policies
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Counts Evidence only when every supplied policy accepts it.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class AllEvidencePolicies :
        EvidenceContributionPolicyBase
    {
        private readonly IReadOnlyList<IEvidenceContributionPolicy> _policies;

        public AllEvidencePolicies(
            IReadOnlyList<IEvidenceContributionPolicy> policies)
        {
            if (policies == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(policies));
            }

            if (policies.Count == 0)
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "At least one policy is required.",
                    nameof(policies));
            }

            var snapshot =
                new List<IEvidenceContributionPolicy>(
                    policies.Count);

            for (var index = 0;
                 index < policies.Count;
                 index++)
            {
                snapshot.Add(
                    policies[index] ??
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Policies cannot contain null.",
                        nameof(policies)));
            }

            _policies =
                new ReadOnlyCollection<IEvidenceContributionPolicy>(
                    snapshot);
        }

        public IReadOnlyList<IEvidenceContributionPolicy> Policies =>
            _policies;

        protected override bool Evaluate(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)
        {
            for (var index = 0;
                 index < _policies.Count;
                 index++)
            {
                if (!_policies[index].Contributes(evidence))
                {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            return true;
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Policies

    /// <summary>

    {

        public AnyEvidencePolicies(
            IReadOnlyList<IEvidenceContributionPolicy> policies)
        {
            if (policies == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(policies));
            }

            if (policies.Count == 0)
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "At least one policy is required.",
                    nameof(policies));
            }

            var snapshot =
                new List<IEvidenceContributionPolicy>(
                    policies.Count);

            for (var index = 0;
                 index < policies.Count;
                 index++)
            {
                snapshot.Add(
                    policies[index] ??
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Policies cannot contain null.",
                        nameof(policies)));
            }

            _policies =
                new ReadOnlyCollection<IEvidenceContributionPolicy>(
                    snapshot);
        }

        public IReadOnlyList<IEvidenceContributionPolicy> Policies =>
            _policies;

        protected override bool Evaluate(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)
        {
            for (var index = 0;
                 index < _policies.Count;
                 index++)
            {
                if (_policies[index].Contributes(evidence))
                {
                    return true;
                }
            }

            return false;
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Policies\BelievabilityRangePolicy.cs
################################################################
using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Policies
{
    public sealed class BelievabilityRangePolicy :
        EvidenceContributionPolicyBase
    {
        public BelievabilityRangePolicy(
            double minimumBelievability,
            double maximumBelievability)
        {
            MinimumBelievability =
                RequireNormalized(
                    minimumBelievability,
                    nameof(minimumBelievability));

            MaximumBelievability =
                RequireNormalized(
                    maximumBelievability,
                    nameof(maximumBelievability));

            if (MinimumBelievability > MaximumBelievability)
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "Minimum Believability cannot exceed maximum Believability.");
            }
        }

        public double MinimumBelievability { get; }
        public double MaximumBelievability { get; }

        protected override bool Evaluate(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)
        {
            var believability =
                evidence.ProofResult.Believability;

            return believability >= MinimumBelievability &&
                   believability <= MaximumBelievability;
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Policies\BelievableFalseEvidencePolicy.cs
################################################################
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Policies
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Counts convincing Evidence whose claim is nevertheless false.
    /// This is the central social-deduction and deception policy.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class BelievableFalseEvidencePolicy :
        EvidenceContributionPolicyBase
    {
        public BelievableFalseEvidencePolicy(
            double minimumBelievability)
        {
            MinimumBelievability =
                RequireNormalized(
                    minimumBelievability,
                    nameof(minimumBelievability));
        }

        public double MinimumBelievability { get; }

        protected override bool Evaluate(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)
        {
            return !evidence.ProofResult.IsTrue &&
                   evidence.ProofResult.Believability >=
                   MinimumBelievability;
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Policies\ConditionEqualsPolicy.cs
################################################################
using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Policies
{
    public sealed class ConditionEqualsPolicy :
        EvidenceContributionPolicyBase
    {
        private readonly object? _expectedValue;

        public ConditionEqualsPolicy(
            string conditionName,
            object? expectedValue)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(conditionName))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "A condition name is required.",
                    nameof(conditionName));
            }

            ConditionName =
                conditionName.Trim();

            _expectedValue =
                expectedValue;
        }

        public string ConditionName { get; }
        public object? ExpectedValue => _expectedValue;

        protected override bool Evaluate(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)
        {
            return evidence.Conditions.TryGetValue(
                       ConditionName,
                       out var actualValue) &&
                   Equals(
                       actualValue,
                       _expectedValue);
        }
    }
}

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Policies

    /// <summary>

    /// Useful at integration boundaries and in certification fixtures.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class DelegateEvidencePolicy :
        EvidenceContributionPolicyBase
    {
        private readonly Func<IDiscoveryEvidence, bool> _predicate;

        public DelegateEvidencePolicy(
            Func<IDiscoveryEvidence, bool> predicate)

            _predicate =

            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Policies\EvidenceContributionPolicyBase.cs
################################################################
using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Policies
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Provides null validation for reusable Evidence contribution policies.
    /// </summary>
    public abstract class EvidenceContributionPolicyBase :
        IEvidenceContributionPolicy
    {
        public bool Contributes(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)
        {
            if (evidence == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(evidence));
            }

            return Evaluate(
                evidence);
        }

        protected abstract bool Evaluate(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence);

        protected static double RequireNormalized(
            double value,
            string parameterName)
        {
            if (double.IsNaN(value) ||
                double.IsInfinity(value) ||
                value < 0.0 ||
                value > 1.0)
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    parameterName,
                    "The value must be between zero and one.");
            }

            return value;
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Policies\FalseEvidencePolicy.cs
################################################################
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Policies
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Counts Evidence whose Proof concludes that the claim is false.
    /// Useful for disproven claims, deception analysis, and contradiction Facts.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class FalseEvidencePolicy :
        EvidenceContributionPolicyBase
    {
        protected override bool Evaluate(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)
        {
            return !evidence.ProofResult.IsTrue;
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Policies\MaximumBelievabilityPolicy.cs
################################################################
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Policies
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Counts Evidence at or below a Believability ceiling.
    /// Useful for doubt, weak-rumor, and suspicious-source Facts.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class MaximumBelievabilityPolicy :
        EvidenceContributionPolicyBase
    {
        public MaximumBelievabilityPolicy(
            double maximumBelievability)
        {
            MaximumBelievability =
                RequireNormalized(
                    maximumBelievability,
                    nameof(maximumBelievability));
        }

        public double MaximumBelievability { get; }

        protected override bool Evaluate(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)
        {
            return evidence.ProofResult.Believability <=
                   MaximumBelievability;
        }
    }
}

using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Policies
{
    public sealed class MinimumStrengthPolicy :
        EvidenceContributionPolicyBase
    {
        public MinimumStrengthPolicy(
            double minimumStrength)
        {
            if (double.IsNaN(minimumStrength) ||
                double.IsInfinity(minimumStrength))
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(minimumStrength),
                    "Minimum strength must be finite.");
            }

            MinimumStrength =
                minimumStrength;
        }

        public double MinimumStrength { get; }

        protected override bool Evaluate(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)
        {
            return evidence.Strength >= MinimumStrength;
        }
    }
}

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Policies

        EvidenceContributionPolicyBase
    {

            IEvidenceContributionPolicy policy)
        {
            _policy =
                policy ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(policy));
        }

        public IEvidenceContributionPolicy Policy =>
            _policy;

        protected override bool Evaluate(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)

using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Policies
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Counts true Evidence that remains below a Believability ceiling.
    /// Useful for dismissed truths and warnings nobody accepts.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class TrueButUnbelievableEvidencePolicy :
        EvidenceContributionPolicyBase
    {
        public TrueButUnbelievableEvidencePolicy(
            double maximumBelievability)
        {
            MaximumBelievability =
                RequireNormalized(
                    maximumBelievability,
                    nameof(maximumBelievability));
        }

        public double MaximumBelievability { get; }

        protected override bool Evaluate(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)
        {
            return evidence.ProofResult.IsTrue &&
                   evidence.ProofResult.Believability <=
                   MaximumBelievability;
        }
    }
}

using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

    /// Counts Evidence whose Proof concludes that the claim is true.
    /// Believability does not affect the decision.
    /// </summary>

        EvidenceContributionPolicyBase
    {
        protected override bool Evaluate(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence)

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Proofs\ProofResult.cs
################################################################
using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Proofs
{
    public sealed class ProofResult :
        IProofResult
    {
        public ProofResult(
            IDiscoveryEvidence evidence,
            IProof proof,
            double believability,
            bool isTrue,
            string? reason = null)
        {
            Evidence =
                evidence ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(evidence));

            Proof =
                proof ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(proof));

            if (double.IsNaN(
                    believability) ||
                double.IsInfinity(
                    believability) ||
                believability < 0.0 ||
                believability > 1.0)
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(believability),
                    "Believability must be between zero and one.");
            }

            Believability =
                believability;

            IsTrue =
                isTrue;

            Reason =
                reason;
        }

        public IDiscoveryEvidence Evidence { get; }

        public IProof Proof { get; }

        public double Believability { get; }

        public bool IsTrue { get; }

        public string? Reason { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Registries\DiscoveryClusterMappingRegistry.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Clusters;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Registries
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryClusterMappingRegistry : IDiscoveryClusterMappingRegistry
    {
        private readonly object _gate = new object();
        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<Guid, IDiscoveryClusterMapping> _byId = new ConcurrentDictionary<Guid, IDiscoveryClusterMapping>();
        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, IDiscoveryClusterMapping> _bySlug = new ConcurrentDictionary<string, IDiscoveryClusterMapping>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, IDiscoveryClusterMapping> _bySemantic = new ConcurrentDictionary<string, IDiscoveryClusterMapping>(StringComparer.Ordinal);

        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryClusterMapping> Mappings
        {
            get
            {
                var result = new List<IDiscoveryClusterMapping>(_byId.Values);
                result.Sort((a, b) => string.CompareOrdinal(a.Slug, b.Slug));
                return result.AsReadOnly();
            }
        }

        public void RegisterDocument(IDiscoveryClusterMappingDocument document)
        {
            if (document == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(document));
            lock (_gate)
            {
                var ids = new HashSet<Guid>();
                var slugs = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
                var keys = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
                foreach (var mapping in document.Mappings)
                {
                    if (mapping.DocumentId != document.Id) throw new InvalidOperationException("Mapping document identity mismatch.");
                    if (!ids.Add(mapping.Id) || _byId.ContainsKey(mapping.Id)) throw new InvalidOperationException("Duplicate cluster ID.");
                    if (!slugs.Add(mapping.Slug) || _bySlug.ContainsKey(mapping.Slug)) throw new InvalidOperationException("Duplicate cluster slug.");
                    if (!keys.Add(mapping.SemanticKey) || _bySemantic.ContainsKey(mapping.SemanticKey)) throw new InvalidOperationException("Duplicate semantic key.");
                }
                foreach (var mapping in document.Mappings)
                {
                    _byId[mapping.Id] = mapping;
                    _bySlug[mapping.Slug] = mapping;
                    _bySemantic[mapping.SemanticKey] = mapping;
                }
            }
        }

        public bool TryGet(Guid clusterId, out IDiscoveryClusterMapping? mapping) => _byId.TryGetValue(clusterId, out mapping);
        public bool TryGet(string clusterSlug, out IDiscoveryClusterMapping? mapping)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(clusterSlug)) { mapping = null; return false; }
            return _bySlug.TryGetValue(clusterSlug, out mapping);
        }
        public bool TryGetBySemanticKey(string semanticKey, out IDiscoveryClusterMapping? mapping)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(semanticKey)) { mapping = null; return false; }
            return _bySemantic.TryGetValue(semanticKey, out mapping);
        }
        public IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryClusterMapping> GetByDocument(Guid documentId)
        {
            var result = new List<IDiscoveryClusterMapping>();
            foreach (var mapping in _byId.Values) if (mapping.DocumentId == documentId) result.Add(mapping);
            result.Sort((a, b) => string.CompareOrdinal(a.Slug, b.Slug));
            return result.AsReadOnly();
        }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Services\SystemWildsServiceClock.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Services;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services
{
    public sealed class SystemWildsServiceClock : IWildsServiceClock
    {
        public DateTimeOffset GetUtcNow() => DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
        public long GetTimestamp() => Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
        public TimeSpan GetElapsedTime(long startingTimestamp, long endingTimestamp)
        {
            var seconds = (double)(endingTimestamp - startingTimestamp) / Stopwatch.Frequency;
            return TimeSpan.FromSeconds(seconds);
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Services\WildsServiceBase.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Services;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services
{
    public abstract class WildsServiceBase<TRequest, TResult> :
        IdentifierBase,
        IWildsService<TRequest, TResult>
        where TRequest : class
        where TResult : class
    {
        private readonly IWildsServiceContextFactory<IWildsServiceContext> _contexts;
        private readonly IWildsServiceExecutionJournal _journal;
        private readonly IWildsServiceClock _clock;
        private int _activeExecutions;

        protected WildsServiceBase(
            string slug,
            IWildsServiceContextFactory<IWildsServiceContext> contexts,
            IWildsServiceExecutionJournal journal,
            IWildsServiceClock clock)
            : base(slug)
        {
            _contexts = contexts ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(contexts));
            _journal = journal ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(journal));
            _clock = clock ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(clock));
        }

        protected WildsServiceBase(
            Guid id,
            string slug,
            IWildsServiceContextFactory<IWildsServiceContext> contexts,
            IWildsServiceExecutionJournal journal,
            IWildsServiceClock clock)
            : base(id, slug)
        {
            _contexts = contexts ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(contexts));
            _journal = journal ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(journal));
            _clock = clock ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(clock));
        }

        public bool IsRunning => Volatile.Read(ref _activeExecutions) > 0;

        public async Task<IWildsServiceResult<TResult>> ExecuteAsync(
            TRequest request,
            object? provenance = null,
            Guid? parentExecutionId = null,
            Guid? pulseId = null,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            if (request == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(request));
            var context = _contexts.Create(this, provenance, parentExecutionId, pulseId, cancellationToken);
            _journal.Record(new WildsServiceExecutionRecord(
                context, null, null, null, WildsServiceExecutionOutcome.Requested));

            Interlocked.Increment(ref _activeExecutions);
            var startedAt = _clock.GetUtcNow();
            var startedStamp = _clock.GetTimestamp();
            _journal.Record(new WildsServiceExecutionRecord(
                context, startedAt, null, null, WildsServiceExecutionOutcome.Running));

            try
            {
                cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                var value = await ExecuteCoreAsync(request, context, cancellationToken);
                cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                if (value == null) throw new InvalidOperationException("A Wilds service cannot return null.");
                var completedAt = _clock.GetUtcNow();
                var duration = _clock.GetElapsedTime(startedStamp, _clock.GetTimestamp());
                _journal.Record(new WildsServiceExecutionRecord(
                    context, startedAt, completedAt, duration, WildsServiceExecutionOutcome.Succeeded));
                return new WildsServiceResult<TResult>(context.Id, value);
            }
            catch (OperationCanceledException) when (cancellationToken.IsCancellationRequested)
            {
                var completedAt = _clock.GetUtcNow();
                var duration = _clock.GetElapsedTime(startedStamp, _clock.GetTimestamp());
                _journal.Record(new WildsServiceExecutionRecord(
                    context, startedAt, completedAt, duration, WildsServiceExecutionOutcome.Cancelled));
                throw;
            }
            catch (Exception exception)
            {
                var completedAt = _clock.GetUtcNow();
                var duration = _clock.GetElapsedTime(startedStamp, _clock.GetTimestamp());
                _journal.Record(new WildsServiceExecutionRecord(
                    context, startedAt, completedAt, duration, WildsServiceExecutionOutcome.Failed,
                    exception.GetType().FullName, exception.Message));
                throw;
            }
            finally
            {
                Interlocked.Decrement(ref _activeExecutions);
            }
        }

        protected abstract Task<TResult> ExecuteCoreAsync(
            TRequest request,
            IWildsServiceContext context,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken);
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Services\WildsServiceCatalog.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Services;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services
{
    public sealed class WildsServiceCatalog : IWildsServiceCatalog
    {
        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<Guid, IIdentifier> _byId = new ConcurrentDictionary<Guid, IIdentifier>();
        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, IIdentifier> _bySlug =
            new ConcurrentDictionary<string, IIdentifier>(StringComparer.Ordinal);

        public IReadOnlyList<IIdentifier> Services => new List<IIdentifier>(_byId.Values).AsReadOnly();

        public void Register(IIdentifier service)
        {
            if (service == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(service));
            if (!_byId.TryAdd(service.Id, service)) throw new InvalidOperationException("A service with this ID is already registered.");
            if (!_bySlug.TryAdd(service.Slug, service))
            {
                _byId.TryRemove(service.Id, out _);
                throw new InvalidOperationException("A service with this slug is already registered.");
            }
        }

        public bool TryGet(Guid serviceId, out IIdentifier? service) => _byId.TryGetValue(serviceId, out service);
        public bool TryGet(string slug, out IIdentifier? service)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(slug)) { service = null; return false; }
            return _bySlug.TryGetValue(slug, out service);
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Threading;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Services;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services
{
    public abstract class WildsServiceContextBase : IdentifierBase, IWildsServiceContext
    {
        protected WildsServiceContextBase(
            Guid id,
            string slug,
            Guid serviceId,
            string serviceSlug,
            Guid? parentExecutionId,
            Guid? pulseId,
            DateTimeOffset requestedAt,
            object? provenance,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
            : base(id, slug)
        {
            if (serviceId == Guid.Empty) throw new ArgumentException("Service context requires a service ID.", nameof(serviceId));
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(serviceSlug)) throw new ArgumentException("Service context requires a service slug.", nameof(serviceSlug));
            ServiceId = serviceId;
            ServiceSlug = serviceSlug.Trim();
            ParentExecutionId = parentExecutionId;
            PulseId = pulseId;
            RequestedAt = requestedAt;
            Provenance = provenance;
            CancellationToken = cancellationToken;
        }

        public Guid ServiceId { get; }
        public string ServiceSlug { get; }
        public Guid? ParentExecutionId { get; }
        public Guid? PulseId { get; }
        public DateTimeOffset RequestedAt { get; }
        public object? Provenance { get; }
        public CancellationToken CancellationToken { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Services\WildsServiceContextFactoryBase.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Threading;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Services;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services
{
    public abstract class WildsServiceContextFactoryBase<TContext> : IWildsServiceContextFactory<TContext>
        where TContext : IWildsServiceContext
    {
        private readonly IWildsServiceClock _clock;

        protected WildsServiceContextFactoryBase(IWildsServiceClock clock)
        {
            _clock = clock ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(clock));
        }

        public TContext Create(
            IIdentifier service,
            object? provenance,
            Guid? parentExecutionId,
            Guid? pulseId,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            if (service == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(service));
            var executionId = Guid.NewGuid();
            return CreateCore(
                executionId,
                "service-execution-" + executionId.ToString("N"),
                service,
                provenance,
                parentExecutionId,
                pulseId,
                _clock.GetUtcNow(),
                cancellationToken);
        }

        protected abstract TContext CreateCore(
            Guid executionId,
            string executionSlug,
            IIdentifier service,
            object? provenance,
            Guid? parentExecutionId,
            Guid? pulseId,
            DateTimeOffset requestedAt,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken);
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Services\WildsServiceExecutionJournal.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Services;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services
{
    public sealed class WildsServiceExecutionJournal : IWildsServiceExecutionJournal
    {
        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<Guid, IWildsServiceExecutionRecord> _records =
            new ConcurrentDictionary<Guid, IWildsServiceExecutionRecord>();

        public void Record(IWildsServiceExecutionRecord record)
        {
            if (record == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(record));
            _records.AddOrUpdate(record.Id, record, (_, __) => record);
        }

        public bool TryGet(Guid executionId, out IWildsServiceExecutionRecord? record) =>
            _records.TryGetValue(executionId, out record);

        public IReadOnlyList<IWildsServiceExecutionRecord> GetByService(Guid serviceId) =>
            Filter(record => record.ServiceId == serviceId);

        public IReadOnlyList<IWildsServiceExecutionRecord> GetByPulse(Guid pulseId) =>
            Filter(record => record.PulseId == pulseId);

        private IReadOnlyList<IWildsServiceExecutionRecord> Filter(Func<IWildsServiceExecutionRecord, bool> predicate)
        {
            var result = new List<IWildsServiceExecutionRecord>();
            foreach (var record in _records.Values)
                if (predicate(record)) result.Add(record);
            result.Sort((left, right) => left.RequestedAt.CompareTo(right.RequestedAt));
            return result.AsReadOnly();
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Services\WildsServiceExecutionRecord.cs
################################################################
using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Services;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Services
{
    public sealed class WildsServiceExecutionRecord : IdentifierBase, IWildsServiceExecutionRecord
    {
        public WildsServiceExecutionRecord(
            IWildsServiceContext context,
            DateTimeOffset? startedAt,
            DateTimeOffset? completedAt,
            TimeSpan? duration,
            WildsServiceExecutionOutcome outcome,
            string? failureType = null,
            string? failureMessage = null)
            : base(context?.Id ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(context)), context.Slug)
        {
            ServiceId = context.ServiceId;
            ServiceSlug = context.ServiceSlug;
            ParentExecutionId = context.ParentExecutionId;
            PulseId = context.PulseId;
            RequestedAt = context.RequestedAt;
            StartedAt = startedAt;
            CompletedAt = completedAt;
            Duration = duration;
            Outcome = outcome;
            Provenance = context.Provenance;
            FailureType = failureType;
            FailureMessage = failureMessage;
        }
        public Guid ServiceId { get; }
        public string ServiceSlug { get; }
        public Guid? ParentExecutionId { get; }
        public Guid? PulseId { get; }
        public DateTimeOffset RequestedAt { get; }
        public DateTimeOffset? StartedAt { get; }
        public DateTimeOffset? CompletedAt { get; }
        public TimeSpan? Duration { get; }
        public WildsServiceExecutionOutcome Outcome { get; }
        public object? Provenance { get; }
        public string? FailureType { get; }
        public string? FailureMessage { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Vectorization\DiscoveryVectorizer.cs
################################################################
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Vectorization;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectors;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectorization
{
    internal sealed class DiscoveryVectorizer : IDiscoveryVectorizer
    {
        private readonly IDiscoveryVectorizationService _service;
        private readonly IDiscoveryVectorMetric _metric;

        public DiscoveryVectorizer(
            IDiscoveryVectorizationService service,
            IDiscoveryVectorMetric metric)
        {
            _service = service ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(service));
            _metric = metric ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(metric));
        }

        public async Task<IDiscoveryVectorizationResult> VectorizeAsync(
            IDiscoveryVectorInput input,
            object? provenance = null,
            Guid? parentExecutionId = null,
            Guid? pulseId = null,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            if (input == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(input));
            var serviceResult = await _service.ExecuteAsync(
                new DiscoveryVectorizationRequest(input),
                provenance,
                parentExecutionId,
                pulseId,
                cancellationToken);

            var contributions = serviceResult.Value.Contributions;
            ValidateExactInput(input, contributions);
            var components = Aggregate(contributions);
            var vector = new DiscoveryVector(
                components,
                contributions,
                new List<IDiscoveryVectorMotion>(),
                _metric,
                DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);
            return new DiscoveryVectorizationResult(serviceResult.ExecutionId, vector);
        }

        private static void ValidateExactInput(
            IDiscoveryVectorInput input,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorContribution> contributions)
        {
            if (contributions.Count == 0)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Vectorization service returned no contributions.");
            for (var index = 0; index < contributions.Count; index++)
                if (!ReferenceEquals(input, contributions[index].Input))
                    throw new InvalidOperationException(
                        "Every vectorization contribution must reference the exact requested input.");
        }

        private static IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorComponent> Aggregate(
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorContribution> contributions)
        {
            var totals = new SortedDictionary<string, double>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
            for (var index = 0; index < contributions.Count; index++)
            {
                var contribution = contributions[index];
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(contribution.Dimension))
                    throw new ArgumentException("Contribution dimension cannot be empty.", nameof(contributions));
                if (double.IsNaN(contribution.Value) || double.IsInfinity(contribution.Value))
                    throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(contributions), "Contribution value must be finite.");
                if (double.IsNaN(contribution.Weight) || double.IsInfinity(contribution.Weight) || contribution.Weight < 0.0)
                    throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(contributions), "Contribution weight must be finite and non-negative.");
                var weighted = contribution.Value * contribution.Weight;
                if (double.IsNaN(weighted) || double.IsInfinity(weighted))
                    throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(contributions), "Weighted contribution must be finite.");
                totals.TryGetValue(contribution.Dimension, out var current);
                var next = current + weighted;
                if (double.IsNaN(next) || double.IsInfinity(next))
                    throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(contributions), "Aggregated component must be finite.");
                totals[contribution.Dimension] = next;
            }
            var components = new List<IDiscoveryVectorComponent>(totals.Count);
            foreach (var pair in totals)
                components.Add(new DiscoveryVectorComponent(pair.Key, pair.Value));
            return components;
        }
    }
}

FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Vectorization\DiscoveryVectorizerFactory.cs
################################################################
using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions.Vectorization;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectorization
{
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorizerFactory : IDiscoveryVectorizerFactory
    {
        public IDiscoveryVectorizer Create(
            IDiscoveryVectorizationService service,
            IDiscoveryVectorMetric metric)
        {
            if (service == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(service));
            if (metric == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(metric));
            return new DiscoveryVectorizer(service, metric);
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Base;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectors
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Concrete immutable evaluation of Discovery geometry.
    ///
    /// Structural validation, immutable snapshots, and magnitude
    /// calculation remain owned by DiscoveryVectorBase.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class DiscoveryVector :
        DiscoveryVectorBase
    {
        public DiscoveryVector(
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorComponent> components,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorContribution> contributions,
            IReadOnlyList<IDiscoveryVectorMotion> motion,
            IDiscoveryVectorMetric metric,
            DateTimeOffset evaluatedAt)
            : base(
                components,
                contributions,
                motion,
                metric,
                evaluatedAt)
        {
        }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Vectors\DiscoveryVectorComponent.cs
################################################################
using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectors
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Concrete immutable coordinate in consumer-defined
    /// Discovery geometry.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorComponent :
        IDiscoveryVectorComponent
    {
        public DiscoveryVectorComponent(
            string name,
            double value)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    name))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "A vector component requires a name.",
                    nameof(name));
            }

            if (double.IsNaN(value) ||
                double.IsInfinity(value))
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(value),
                    "A vector component value must be finite.");
            }

            Name =
                name.Trim();

            Value =
                value;
        }

        public string Name { get; }

        public double Value { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Vectors\DiscoveryVectorContribution.cs
################################################################
using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectors
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Concrete consumer-authorized contribution into one
    /// Discovery-vector dimension.
    ///
    /// The contribution preserves the exact vector input and,
    /// when applicable, the exact Fact from that Discovery that
    /// produced the contribution.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorContribution :
        IDiscoveryVectorContribution
    {
        public DiscoveryVectorContribution(
            IDiscoveryVectorInput input,
            string dimension,
            double value,
            double weight,
            IFact? fact = null)
        {
            Input =
                input ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(input));

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    dimension))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "A vector contribution requires a dimension.",
                    nameof(dimension));
            }

            if (double.IsNaN(value) ||
                double.IsInfinity(value))
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(value),
                    "A vector contribution value must be finite.");
            }

            if (double.IsNaN(weight) ||
                double.IsInfinity(weight))
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(weight),
                    "A vector contribution weight must be finite.");
            }

            if (fact != null &&
                !ReferenceEquals(
                    fact.Entity,
                    Input.Discovery.Entity))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "A contributing Fact must belong to the exact " +
                    "Entity that owns the authoritative Discovery.",
                    nameof(fact));
            }

            Dimension =
                dimension.Trim();

            Value =
                value;

            Weight =
                weight;

            Fact =
                fact;
        }

        public IDiscoveryVectorInput Input { get; }

        public IFact? Fact { get; }

        public string Dimension { get; }

        public double Value { get; }

        public double Weight { get; }
    }
}

################################################################
FILE: WildsEngine.Discoveries\Vectors\DiscoveryVectorInput.cs
################################################################
using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectors
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Concrete authoritative input selected for Discovery
    /// vectorization.
    ///
    /// The Discovery remains authoritative. Provenance is
    /// preserved opaquely and is not interpreted by Discoveries.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorInput :
        IDiscoveryVectorInput
    {
        public DiscoveryVectorInput(
            IDiscovery discovery,
            object? provenance = null)
        {
            Discovery =
                discovery ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(discovery));

            Provenance =
                provenance;
        }

        public IDiscovery Discovery { get; }

        public object? Provenance { get; }
    }
}

using System;
using WildsEngine.Discoveries.Abstractions;

namespace WildsEngine.Discoveries.Vectors
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Concrete temporal movement along one consumer-defined
    /// Discovery dimension.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class DiscoveryVectorMotion :
        IDiscoveryVectorMotion
    {
        public DiscoveryVectorMotion(
            string dimension,
            double velocity,
            double acceleration)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    dimension))
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "Vector motion requires a dimension.",
                    nameof(dimension));
            }

            if (double.IsNaN(velocity) ||
                double.IsInfinity(velocity))
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(velocity),
                    "Velocity must be finite.");
            }

            if (double.IsNaN(acceleration) ||
                double.IsInfinity(acceleration))
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(acceleration),
                    "Acceleration must be finite.");
            }

            Dimension =
                dimension.Trim();

            Velocity =
                velocity;

            Acceleration =
                acceleration;
        }

        public string Dimension { get; }

        public double Velocity { get; }

        public double Acceleration { get; }
    }
}

S1 CLASSIFICATION WORKSHEET
============================================================
Classify each consumer-relevant type as one primary category:
  OPTION       - instance/runtime configuration
  DEFINITION   - consumer-authored structure or relationship
  POLICY       - consumer-owned decision rule
  SERVICE      - consumer or framework processing boundary
  PROFILE      - typed composition for one capability/use case
  REGISTRATION - immutable installed profile/configuration identity
  RESULT       - attributable consumer-facing output
  INTERNAL     - machinery hidden behind the Discoveries surface

Required S1 outputs:
  S1A - classified current surface inventory
  S1B - strongly typed profile model and validation
  S1C - immutable/versioned registration model
  S1D - intentional WildsDiscoveries consumer surface
